FILE: frontend\src\pages\Results.tsx
============================================================
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAsyncData } from "@/hooks/useAsyncData";
import { evaluationService } from "@/services/evaluation";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";

export function Results() {
  const { sessionId } = useParams<{ sessionId: string }>();
  const { data, loading, error } = useAsyncData(
    () => evaluationService.getResults(sessionId as string),
    [sessionId],
  );

  if (loading) return <Spinner label="Loading results" />;
  if (error) return <ErrorBanner message={error} />;
  if (!data) return null;

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1>Your results</h1>
        <div className="score-pill">{data.actualScore}</div>
      </div>

      <Card title="Overall assessment">
        <p>{data.overallAssessment}</p>
      </Card>

      <div className="results-grid">
        <Card title="Strengths">
          <ul className="tag-list tag-list-positive">
            {data.strengths.map((s) => (
              <li key={s}>{s}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
        <Card title="Weaknesses">
          <ul className="tag-list tag-list-negative">
            {data.weaknesses.map((w) => (
              <li key={w}>{w}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Card title="Improvement areas">
        <ul className="plain-list">
          {data.improvementAreas.map((a) => (
            <li key={a}>{a}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Card>

      <Card title="Recommendations">
        <ul className="plain-list">
          {data.actionableRecommendations.map((r) => (
            <li key={r}>{r}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Card>

      <div className="results-actions">
        <Link to="/history">
          <Button variant="secondary">Back to history</Button>
        </Link>
        <Link to="/interview/setup" state={{ config: undefined }}>
          <Button>Practice again</Button>
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\services\auth.ts
============================================================
import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type { AuthResponse, LoginRequest, RegisterRequest } from "@/types/auth";

// Identity Service. The frontend collects credentials and renders whatever
// the backend decides â€” it never determines auth state itself beyond
// reflecting the last response (see hooks/useAuth.tsx).
export const authService = {
  register: (data: RegisterRequest) =>
    apiRequest<AuthResponse>("/api/auth/register", { method: "POST", body: data }),

  login: (data: LoginRequest) =>
    apiRequest<AuthResponse>("/api/auth/login", { method: "POST", body: data }),

  logout: () => apiRequest<void>("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }),

  // Called once on app load so a valid session cookie survives a page
  // refresh instead of bouncing the user back to /login. A 401 here just
  // means "not logged in" â€” see useAuth's bootstrap effect.
  me: () => apiRequest<AuthResponse>("/api/auth/me"),
};



============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\services\evaluation.ts
============================================================
import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type { EvaluationResult } from "@/types/evaluation";

interface EvaluationResponse {
  evaluation: EvaluationResult;
}

// Evaluation Boundary. Starting evaluation and reading results are separate
// calls per the documented API surface â€” the frontend never computes a
// score itself.
export const evaluationService = {
  evaluate: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<EvaluationResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/evaluate`,
      {
        method: "POST",
      },
    ).then(({ evaluation }) => evaluation),

  getResults: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<EvaluationResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/results`,
    ).then(({ evaluation }) => evaluation),
};



============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\services\history.ts
============================================================
import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type { HistoryProgressResponse } from "@/types/history";

// History / Progress Boundary.
export const historyService = {
  getHistory: () =>
    apiRequest<HistoryProgressResponse>("/api/interview-history"),

  getProgress: () =>
    apiRequest<HistoryProgressResponse>("/api/interview-history").then(
      ({ progress }) => progress,
    ),
};



============================================================
FILE: frontend\src\services\http.ts
============================================================
// Single point of contact with the Fastify backend. Every service module
// goes through this â€” never call fetch() directly from a page or hook.
//
// Auth model assumption: the backend issues an httpOnly session cookie on
// login (not a token the frontend stores/attaches). `credentials: "include"`
// is set on every request so that cookie always rides along. If the actual
// backend instead returns a bearer token, swap that in here â€” it's the only
// file that needs to change.

const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? "";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  body: unknown;

  constructor(status: number, message: string, body?: unknown) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.body = body;
  }
}

interface RequestOptions {
  method?: "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";
  body?: unknown;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export async function apiRequest<TResponse>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<TResponse> {
  const { method = "GET", body, signal } = options;
  const requestBody = body ?? (method === "POST" ? {} : undefined);

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    credentials: "include",
    headers: requestBody ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : undefined,
    body: requestBody ? JSON.stringify(requestBody) : undefined,
    signal,
  });

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  const isJson = contentType.includes("application/json");
  const payload = isJson
    ? await response.json().catch(() => null)
    : await response.text().catch(() => null);

  if (!response.ok) {
    // The backend is the source of truth for error shape/messages. Adjust
    // this extraction once the real error envelope is confirmed.
    const message =
      (isJson && payload && typeof payload === "object" && "error" in payload
        ? String((payload as { error: unknown }).error)
        : isJson &&
            payload &&
            typeof payload === "object" &&
            "message" in payload
          ? String((payload as { message: unknown }).message)
          : null) ?? `Request failed with status ${response.status}`;
    throw new ApiError(response.status, message, payload);
  }

  return payload as TResponse;
}

import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type {
  ExpectedScoreRequest,
  InterviewSession,
  InterviewSessionConfig,
  SubmitAnswerRequest,
} from "@/types/interview";

interface SessionResponse {
  session: InterviewSession;
}

// Session Engine. Every call here returns the session state as the backend
// currently sees it â€” pages render that state, they do not compute the next
// state client-side.
export const interviewService = {
  createSession: (config: InterviewSessionConfig) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>("/api/interview-sessions", {
      method: "POST",
      body: config,
    }).then(({ session }) => session),

  getSession: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(`/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}`).then(
      ({ session }) => session,
    ),

  submitAnswer: (sessionId: string, data: SubmitAnswerRequest) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/answers`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        body: data,
      },
    ).then(({ session }) => session),

  submitClosing: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(`/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/close`, {
      method: "POST",
    }).then(({ session }) => session),

  // Expected score is the candidate's self-assessment only â€” it is stored,
  // never fed into the evaluation algorithm as an input.
  submitExpectedScore: (sessionId: string, data: ExpectedScoreRequest) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/expected-score`,
      { method: "POST", body: data },
    ).then(({ session }) => session),

  terminate: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/terminate`,
      {
        method: "POST",
      },
    ).then(({ session }) => session),
};

FILE: frontend\src\services\microphone.ts
============================================================
export interface MicrophoneCapture {
  start(): Promise<void>;
  stop(): void;
  isActive(): boolean;
  onAudio(handler: (audio: ArrayBuffer) => void): void;
}

export function createMicrophoneCapture(): MicrophoneCapture {
  let stream: MediaStream | null = null;
  let audioContext: AudioContext | null = null;
  let source: MediaStreamAudioSourceNode | null = null;
  let processor: ScriptProcessorNode | null = null;
  let active = false;
  let audioHandler: ((audio: ArrayBuffer) => void) | null = null;

  return {
    async start() {
      if (active) return;

      stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        audio: {
          echoCancellation: true,
          noiseSuppression: true,
          autoGainControl: true,
        },
      });

      audioContext = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 16000 });
      source = audioContext.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
      processor = audioContext.createScriptProcessor(2048, 1, 1);

      processor.onaudioprocess = (event) => {
        if (!active || !audioHandler) return;

        const input = event.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0);
        const pcm = new Int16Array(input.length);

        for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i += 1) {
          const sample = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, input[i]));
          pcm[i] = sample < 0 ? sample * 0x8000 : sample * 0x7fff;
        }

        audioHandler(pcm.buffer);
      };

      source.connect(processor);
      processor.connect(audioContext.destination);
      active = true;
    },

    stop() {
      active = false;

      processor?.disconnect();
      source?.disconnect();
      stream?.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());

      void audioContext?.close();

      processor = null;
      source = null;
      stream = null;
      audioContext = null;
    },

    isActive() {
      return active;
    },

    onAudio(handler) {
      audioHandler = handler;
    },
  };
}

FILE: frontend\src\services\voiceSession.ts
============================================================
import type {
  VoiceErrorCode,
  VoiceSession,
  VoiceState,
  VoiceStateSnapshot,
} from "@/types/voice";
import { createMicrophoneCapture } from "./microphone";

export interface VoiceSessionOptions {
  url: string;
  sessionId: string;
  onStateChange?: (snapshot: VoiceStateSnapshot) => void;
  onTranscript?: (transcript: string, isFinal: boolean) => void;
  onError?: (code: VoiceErrorCode, message: string) => void;
}

export function createVoiceSession(
  options: VoiceSessionOptions,
): VoiceSession {
  const microphone = createMicrophoneCapture();

  let socket: WebSocket | null = null;
  let state: VoiceState = "idle";
  let errorCode: VoiceErrorCode | undefined;
  let errorMessage: string | undefined;

  const updateState = (
    nextState: VoiceState,
    nextErrorCode?: VoiceErrorCode,
    nextErrorMessage?: string,
  ) => {
    state = nextState;
    errorCode = nextErrorCode;
    errorMessage = nextErrorMessage;

    options.onStateChange?.({
      state,
      ...(errorCode ? { errorCode } : {}),
      ...(errorMessage ? { errorMessage } : {}),
    });
  };

  microphone.onAudio((audio) => {
    if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      socket.send(audio);
    }
  });

  return {
    async connect() {
      if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) return;

      updateState("connecting");

      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const separator = options.url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
        socket = new WebSocket(`${options.url}${separator}sessionId=${encodeURIComponent(options.sessionId)}`);

        socket.onopen = () => {
          updateState("listening");
          resolve();
        };

        socket.onerror = () => {
          updateState(
            "error",
            "CONNECTION_FAILED",
            "Voice connection failed.",
          );
          options.onError?.(
            "CONNECTION_FAILED",
            "Voice connection failed.",
          );
          reject(new Error("Voice connection failed"));
        };

        socket.onclose = () => {
          if (state !== "error") {
            updateState("disconnected");
          }
        };

        socket.onmessage = (event) => {
          try {
            const message = JSON.parse(event.data);

            if (message.type === "state") {
              updateState(message.state);
            } else if (message.type === "transcription") {
              options.onTranscript?.(
                message.transcript,
                Boolean(message.isFinal),
              );
            } else if (message.type === "error") {
              updateState(
                "error",
                message.code,
                message.message,
              );
              options.onError?.(message.code, message.message);
            } else if (message.type === "interrupted") {
              updateState("interrupted");
            }
          } catch {
            updateState(
              "error",
              "CONNECTION_FAILED",
              "Invalid voice message.",
            );
          }
        };
      });
    },

    async disconnect() {
      microphone.stop();
      socket?.close();
      socket = null;
      updateState("disconnected");
    },

    async startListening() {
      if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
        throw new Error("Voice session is not connected");
      }

      await microphone.start();
      updateState("listening");
    },

    async stopListening() {
      microphone.stop();

      if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        socket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "stopListening" }));
      }

      updateState("processing");
    },

    async interrupt() {
      microphone.stop();

      if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        socket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "interrupt" }));
      }

      updateState("interrupted");
    },

    getState() {
      return {
        state,
        ...(errorCode ? { errorCode } : {}),
        ...(errorMessage ? { errorMessage } : {}),
      };
    },
  };
}

// Session Engine owns interview session, configuration, current question,
// candidate answers, answer analysis state, follow-up, duration, completion,
// early exit, and closing. The frontend only displays whatever state comes
// back â€” it must never invent a transition between these states itself.
export type InterviewLifecycleState =
  | "not_started"
  | "active"
  | "closing"
  | "completed"
  | "terminated";

export type InterviewType = string;
export type InterviewLanguage = "Hinglish" | "English" | "Hindi";
// Only "Text" is implemented server-side today per the architecture doc;
// the type is kept open in case voice/mixed modes land later.
export type InterviewMode = string;

export interface InterviewSessionConfig {
  interviewType: InterviewType;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  duration: number; // minutes
  language: InterviewLanguage;
  mode: InterviewMode;
}

export interface AnswerAnalysis {
  category: "strong" | "partial" | "weak" | "irrelevant" | "unclear";
  technicalCorrectness: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  relevance: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  completeness: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  explanationQuality: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  reasoning: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  strengths: string[];
  missingImportantPoints: string[];
  improvementOpportunities: string[];
  analysisNotes: string;
}

// Generic envelope for GET /api/interview-sessions/:sessionId and any
// response that carries updated session state. Treat this as a starting
// point â€” inspect the backend's actual response body and correct field
// names/nesting before relying on it.
export interface InterviewSession {
  id: string;
  accountId: string;
  interviewType: string;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  duration: number;
  language: string;
  mode: string;
  state: InterviewLifecycleState;
  currentQuestion: string | null;
  pendingFollowUpQuestion: string | null;
  questionIndex: number;
  turnCount: number;
  expectedScore: number | null;
  lastTurnAnalysisStatus: "pending" | "completed" | "failed" | null;
  lastTurnAnalysis: AnswerAnalysis | null;
  startedAt: string;
  completedAt: string | null;
  // Phase 1.5/1.6 (answer analysis + follow-up decision) exist on the
  // backend already, but the exact field names/nesting for what the
  // session response exposes about the last analysis are NOT verified
  // against the real backend yet â€” this is a placeholder shape so the UI
  // has somewhere to show it once confirmed. Update to match the actual
  // response before relying on it.
  lastAnalysisSummary?: string | null;
}

export interface SubmitAnswerRequest {
  answer: string;
}

export interface ExpectedScoreRequest {
  expectedScore: number;
}

FILE: frontend\README.md

# I-RIS Frontend

React + TypeScript + Vite, built strictly from the documented architecture:
one page per documented route, one service module per backend boundary
(Identity, Session Engine, Evaluation, History/Progress), and no client-side
invention of interview state transitions â€” every page renders whatever
`state` the backend last reported.

## Wiring into your existing workspace

Your repo already uses npm workspaces with a `frontend` and `backend`
package. Drop this folder in as the `frontend` workspace (replace or merge
with what's there):

```
your-repo/
  backend/
  frontend/   â† this folder
  package.json  (root, with "workspaces": ["backend", "frontend"])
```

Then, per your documented dev workflow:

```bash
npm install
npm run dev --workspace backend
npm run dev --workspace frontend   # in a second terminal
```

The Vite dev server proxies `/api/*` to the backend (see `vite.config.ts`,
default target `http://localhost:4000` â€” change `VITE_BACKEND_ORIGIN` if
your backend runs elsewhere), so the frontend never needs CORS config in
development.

## Fixed for this integration pass

- **Backend port** â€” dev proxy target is now `http://localhost:3000`
  (`vite.config.ts`), matching the actual I-RIS backend instead of the
  earlier `4000` placeholder.
- **Auth persistence** â€” `useAuth` now calls `GET /api/auth/me` once on
  app load (`authService.me()` in `src/services/auth.ts`) and
  `ProtectedRoute` waits out that check (a `"checking"` status) before
  deciding whether to redirect to `/login`. A page refresh with a valid
  session cookie now stays logged in instead of bouncing to `/login`.
- **Dashboard / interview screen layout** â€” reworked per the integration
  notes: the dashboard leads with a single "Start a focused interview"
  action plus Recent practice / Current progress side by side, and the
  interview screen is a structured question/response workspace (Question
  block â†’ Your response block â†’ centered Submit), not a chat thread. A
  placeholder `lastAnalysisSummary` field on `InterviewSession`
  (`src/types/interview.ts`) gives the follow-up state somewhere to show
  what the backend said about the previous answer â€” see the next section.

## Still needs the actual backend source â€” not guessed further

Per the integration notes: no new endpoints get invented to fill gaps: the
following need the real backend inspected and the code updated to match,
not another guess from this side.

1. **API contract types** (`src/types/*.ts`) â€” `AuthResponse`,
   `InterviewSession`, `EvaluationResult`, etc. are still a best-effort
   reconstruction from the architecture doc. Share the backend's actual
   route handlers/response schemas (or the repo itself) and these get
   corrected to match exactly, field by field.
2. **Progress endpoint** (`src/services/history.ts`) â€” `getProgress()`
   still calls a guessed `GET /api/progress`. Phase 1.9 already implements
   this on the backend; point this at the real route once known.
3. **Session state names** (`src/types/interview.ts`,
   `InterviewLifecycleState`) â€” the eleven states here
   (`READY` â†’ â€¦ â†’ `RESULTS`) come from the architecture doc, not a
   confirmed dump of the Session Engine's actual state names. If the
   backend uses different names or nesting, the frontend must adopt those,
   not the other way round.
4. **Answer analysis / follow-up payload** â€” `lastAnalysisSummary` on
   `InterviewSession` is a placeholder so the UI has somewhere to render
   Phase 1.5/1.6's analysis output; the real field name(s) and whether
   it's nested under something else need confirming against the backend
   response before this is more than a stub.

The cleanest way to close these out: point me at (or paste in) the actual
backend route handlers and response shapes â€” or the repo itself â€” and
these get filled in precisely instead of re-guessed.

## Structure

```
src/
  app/        router + route protection
  components/ common UI, navigation shell, feedback, interview-specific
  pages/      one file per documented route
  services/   one module per backend boundary â€” the ONLY files that call fetch
  hooks/      useAuth (identity context), useAsyncData (fetch-on-mount)
  types/      request/response shapes per boundary
  styles/     single global stylesheet (dark UI, Space Grotesk + Roboto)
```

Design direction: dark surface, warm amber accent, Space Grotesk for
headings and Roboto for body â€” matches the "private interview journal, not
social media" feel from the product notes, and your stated preference for
dark, professional SaaS dashboards.

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactElement } from "react";
import {
  Dashboard,
  type DashboardAction,
  type DashboardUser,
} from "./components/Dashboard";
import {
  InterviewConfiguration,
  initialConfiguration,
  type ConfigurationAction,
  type InterviewConfiguration as InterviewConfigurationState,
} from "./components/InterviewConfiguration";
import {
  hasValidConfiguration,
  ReadyScreen,
  type ReadyAction,
} from "./components/ReadyScreen";
import { InterviewSessionEntry } from "./components/InterviewSessionEntry";
import { ActiveInterview } from "./components/ActiveInterview";
import {
  HistoryProgress,
  type HistoryItem,
  type Progress,
} from "./components/HistoryProgress";
import {
  OverviewIcon,
  StartIcon,
  HistoryIcon,
  ProgressIcon,
} from "./components/icons";

const apiBaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? "http://localhost:3001";

type User = DashboardUser;
type AuthMode = "login" | "register";

export function getPageForPath(pathname: string): string {
  const pages: Record<string, string> = {
    "/": "Entry",
    "/account": "Account Access",
    "/dashboard": "Dashboard",
    "/interview/setup": "Interview Setup",
    "/ready": "Ready",
    "/interview": "Interview",
    "/results": "Results",
    "/history": "History",
    "/progress": "Progress",
  };

  return pages[pathname] ?? "Entry";
}

export function App(): ReactElement {
  const [health, setHealth] = useState("Checking backend...");
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [authChecked, setAuthChecked] = useState(false);
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState<AuthMode>("login");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(getPageForPath(window.location.pathname));
  const [configuration, setConfiguration] =
    useState<InterviewConfigurationState>(initialConfiguration);
  const [sessionId, setSessionId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [recentHistory, setRecentHistory] = useState<HistoryItem[]>([]);
  const [recentProgress, setRecentProgress] = useState<Progress | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/health`)
      .then((response) => response.json())
      .then((result: { status?: string; database?: string }) => {
        setHealth(
          result.status === "ok" && result.database === "connected"
            ? "Backend and database connected"
            : "Backend unavailable",
        );
      })
      .catch(() => setHealth("Backend unavailable"));

    fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/auth/me`, { credentials: "include" })
      .then(async (response) =>
        response.ok ? ((await response.json()).user as User) : null,
      )
      .then((authenticatedUser) => {
        setUser(authenticatedUser);
        setAuthChecked(true);
      })
      .catch(() => setAuthChecked(true));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-history`, { credentials: "include" })
      .then(async (response) =>
        response.ok
          ? ((await response.json()) as {
              history?: HistoryItem[];
              progress?: Progress;
            })
          : null,
      )
      .then((result) => {
        setRecentHistory(result?.history ?? []);
        setRecentProgress(result?.progress ?? null);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setRecentHistory([]);
        setRecentProgress(null);
      });
  }, [user, page]);

  function navigate(path: string): void {
    window.history.pushState({}, "", path);
    setPage(getPageForPath(path));
    setMessage("");
  }

  async function submitAuth(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>): Promise<void> {
    event.preventDefault();
    setMessage("");
    const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/auth/${authMode}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      credentials: "include",
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
    });
    const result = (await response.json()) as { user?: User; error?: string };
    if (!response.ok || !result.user) {
      setMessage(result.error ?? "Account access could not be completed.");
      return;
    }
    setUser(result.user);
    setPassword("");
    navigate("/dashboard");
  }

  async function logout(): Promise<void> {
    await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/auth/logout`, {
      method: "POST",
      credentials: "include",
    });
    setUser(null);
    navigate("/account");
  }

  function handleDashboardAction(action: DashboardAction): void {
    if (action === "new-interview") {
      navigate("/interview/setup");
      return;
    }
    if (action === "history") {
      navigate("/history");
      return;
    }
    navigate("/progress");
  }

  function handleConfigurationAction(
    action: ConfigurationAction,
    _configuration?: InterviewConfigurationState,
  ): void {
    if (action === "back") {
      navigate("/dashboard");
      return;
    }
    if (configuration) navigate("/ready");
  }

  async function handleReadyAction(action: ReadyAction): Promise<void> {
    if (action === "edit") navigate("/interview/setup");
    if (action === "start") {
      const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        credentials: "include",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...configuration,
          duration: Number(configuration.duration),
        }),
      });
      const result = (await response.json()) as {
        session?: { id: string };
        error?: string;
      };
      if (!response.ok || !result.session) {
        setMessage(result.error ?? "Interview could not be started.");
        return;
      }
      setSessionId(result.session.id);
      navigate("/interview");
    }
  }

  if (!authChecked) {
    return (
      <main className="shell">
        <p className="status">Checking account access...</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <main className="shell">
        <header className="header">
          <div>
            <p className="eyebrow">Interview Response Intelligence System</p>
            <h1>I-RIS</h1>
          </div>
          <span className="status">{health}</span>
        </header>
        <section className="content">
          <p className="eyebrow">
            {page === "Entry" ? "Practice with purpose" : "Account access"}
          </p>
          <h2>
            {page === "Entry"
              ? "Practice Interviews. Improve Every Time."
              : authMode === "login"
                ? "Welcome back"
                : "Create your account"}
          </h2>
          {page === "Entry" ? (
            <>
              <p>
                Build confidence through realistic technical interview practice
                and structured feedback.
              </p>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate("/account")}>
                Continue to account access
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={submitAuth}>
              <label>
                Email
                <input
                  type="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
                  required
                  autoComplete="email"
                />
              </label>
              <label>
                Password
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                  required
                  autoComplete={
                    authMode === "login" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
                  }
                />
              </label>
              <button type="submit">
                {authMode === "login" ? "Continue" : "Create account"}
              </button>
              {message && (
                <p className="error" role="alert">
                  {message}
                </p>
              )}
              <button
                className="link-button"
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setAuthMode(authMode === "login" ? "register" : "login");
                  setMessage("");
                }}
              >
                {authMode === "login"
                  ? "Create a new account"
                  : "Use an existing account"}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="app-shell">
      <aside className="app-rail" aria-label="Primary navigation">
        <button
          className="rail-brand"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}
          aria-label="Go to dashboard"
        >
          <span className="brand-mark">IR</span>
          <span>I-RIS</span>
        </button>
        <p className="rail-label">Workspace</p>
        <nav className="rail-nav">
          {[
            {
              label: "Overview",
              path: "/dashboard",
              icon: <OverviewIcon />,
              active: page === "Dashboard",
            },
            {
              label: "Start interview",
              path: "/interview/setup",
              icon: <StartIcon />,
              active:
                page === "Interview Setup" ||
                page === "Ready" ||
                page === "Interview",
            },
            {
              label: "History",
              path: "/history",
              icon: <HistoryIcon />,
              active: page === "History",
            },
            {
              label: "Progress",
              path: "/progress",
              icon: <ProgressIcon />,
              active: page === "Progress",
            },
          ].map((item) => (
            <a
              key={item.path}
              className={item.active ? "is-active" : ""}
              href={item.path}
              aria-current={item.active ? "page" : undefined}
              onClick={(event) => {
                event.preventDefault();
                navigate(item.path);
              }}
            >
              {item.icon}
              {item.label}
            </a>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="rail-account">
          <span className="account-dot" aria-hidden="true">
            {user.email.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
          </span>
          <span className="account-email">{user.email}</span>
          <button type="button" onClick={logout}>
            Log out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <section className="workspace">
        <header className="workspace-header">
          <div>
            <p className="workspace-breadcrumb">I-RIS / {page}</p>
            <p className="workspace-health">
              <span />
              System ready for practice
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="workspace-context">
            Interview Response Intelligence System
          </div>
        </header>
        <div className="workspace-content">
          {page === "Interview Setup" ? (
            <InterviewConfiguration
              configuration={configuration}
              onChange={setConfiguration}
              onAction={handleConfigurationAction}
            />
          ) : page === "Ready" && hasValidConfiguration(configuration) ? (
            <ReadyScreen
              configuration={configuration}
              onAction={handleReadyAction}
            />
          ) : page === "Interview" && sessionId ? (
            <ActiveInterview apiBaseUrl={apiBaseUrl} sessionId={sessionId} />
          ) : page === "History" || page === "Progress" ? (
            <HistoryProgress
              apiBaseUrl={apiBaseUrl}
              initialView={page === "Progress" ? "progress" : "history"}
              onRePractice={() => navigate("/interview/setup")}
            />
          ) : page === "Interview" ? (
            <InterviewSessionEntry />
          ) : (
            <>
              <Dashboard
                user={user}
                history={recentHistory}
                progress={recentProgress}
                onAction={handleDashboardAction}
              />
              {message && (
                <p className="dashboard-message" role="status">
                  {message}
                </p>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";

// Route protection is a UX convenience only â€” it is not the security
// boundary. The backend must reject unauthenticated/unauthorized requests
// on every protected endpoint regardless of what this component does.
//
// "checking" is the brief window while GET /api/auth/me is in flight on
// app load (see useAuth) â€” waiting it out here is what stops a valid
// session from being bounced to /login on every page refresh.
export function ProtectedRoute() {
  const { user, status } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (status === "checking") {
    return <Spinner label="Checking session" />;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" state={{ from: location }} replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

import { createBrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/navigation/AppShell";
import { ProtectedRoute } from "./ProtectedRoute";
import { Login } from "@/pages/Login";
import { Register } from "@/pages/Register";
import { Dashboard } from "@/pages/Dashboard";
import { InterviewSetup } from "@/pages/InterviewSetup";
import { Ready } from "@/pages/Ready";
import { ActiveInterview } from "@/pages/ActiveInterview";
import { Results } from "@/pages/Results";
import { History } from "@/pages/History";
import { Progress } from "@/pages/Progress";

// Mirrors the route list from the architecture doc 1:1. Nothing here decides
// interview lifecycle â€” /interview/:sessionId just renders whatever state
// the backend reports for that session.
export const router = createBrowserRouter([
  { path: "/login", element: <Login /> },
  { path: "/register", element: <Register /> },
  {
    element: <ProtectedRoute />,
    children: [
      {
        element: <AppShell />,
        children: [
          { path: "/", element: <Dashboard /> },
          { path: "/dashboard", element: <Dashboard /> },
          { path: "/interview/setup", element: <InterviewSetup /> },
          { path: "/interview/ready", element: <Ready /> },
          { path: "/interview/:sessionId", element: <ActiveInterview /> },
          { path: "/interview/:sessionId/results", element: <Results /> },
          { path: "/history", element: <History /> },
          { path: "/progress", element: <Progress /> },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
]);

  it("keeps Step 6 text session states separate from evaluation", () => {
    const states = ["active", "completed", "terminated"];
    expect(states).toEqual(["active", "completed", "terminated"]);
    expect(states).not.toContain("evaluated");
  });

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactElement } from "react";

type AnalysisCategory =
  | "strong"
  | "partial"
  | "weak"
  | "irrelevant"
  | "unclear";

type AnalysisBlock = {
  assessment: string;
  uncertain: boolean;
};

type SessionAnalysis = {
  category: AnalysisCategory;
  technicalCorrectness: AnalysisBlock;
  relevance: AnalysisBlock;
  completeness: AnalysisBlock;
  explanationQuality: AnalysisBlock;
  reasoning: AnalysisBlock;
  strengths: string[];
  missingImportantPoints: string[];
  improvementOpportunities: string[];
  analysisNotes: string;
};

type Session = {
  id: string;
  interviewType: string;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  duration: number;
  language: string;
  mode: string;
  state: "active" | "closing" | "completed" | "terminated";
  currentQuestion: string | null;
  pendingFollowUpQuestion: string | null;
  turnCount: number;
  expectedScore: number | null;
  lastTurnAnalysisStatus: "pending" | "completed" | "failed" | null;
  lastTurnAnalysis: SessionAnalysis | null;
};

type EvaluationResult = {
  actualScore: number;
  overallAssessment: string;
  strengths: string[];
  weaknesses: string[];
  improvementAreas: string[];
  actionableRecommendations: string[];
  evidenceSummary: string;
  generatedAt: string;
};

type ActiveInterviewProps = {
  apiBaseUrl: string;
  sessionId: string;
};

export function ActiveInterview({
  apiBaseUrl,
  sessionId,
}: ActiveInterviewProps): ReactElement {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [closingScore, setClosingScore] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submittingScore, setSubmittingScore] = useState(false);
  const [evaluation, setEvaluation] = useState<EvaluationResult | null>(null);
  const [loadingResults, setLoadingResults] = useState(false);

  async function loadSession(): Promise<void> {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}`,
      { credentials: "include" },
    );
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      session?: Session;
      error?: string;
    };
    if (!response.ok || !result.session) {
      setError(result.error ?? "Interview session could not be loaded.");
    } else {
      setSession(result.session);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadSession();
  }, [sessionId]);

  async function submitAnswer(
    event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  ): Promise<void> {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!answer.trim()) {
      setError("Answer cannot be empty.");
      return;
    }
    setError("");
    setSubmitting(true);
    const response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/answers`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        credentials: "include",
        body: JSON.stringify({ answer }),
      },
    );
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      session?: Session;
      error?: string;
    };
    setSubmitting(false);
    if (!response.ok || !result.session) {
      setError(result.error ?? "Answer could not be submitted.");
      return;
    }
    setSession(result.session);
    setAnswer("");
  }

  async function terminate(): Promise<void> {
    setError("");
    const response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/close`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
      },
    );
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      session?: Session;
      error?: string;
    };
    if (!response.ok || !result.session) {
      setError(result.error ?? "Interview could not be closed.");
      return;
    }
    setSession(result.session);
  }

  async function submitExpectedScore(): Promise<void> {
    const numericScore = Number(closingScore);
    if (
      !Number.isFinite(numericScore) ||
      numericScore < 0 ||
      numericScore > 100
    ) {
      setError("Provide a valid expected score from 0 to 100.");
      return;
    }
    setError("");
    setSubmittingScore(true);
    const response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/expected-score`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        credentials: "include",
        body: JSON.stringify({ expectedScore: numericScore }),
      },
    );
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      session?: Session;
      error?: string;
    };
    setSubmittingScore(false);
    if (!response.ok || !result.session) {
      setError(result.error ?? "Expected score could not be submitted.");
      return;
    }
    setSession(result.session);
    setClosingScore("");
  }

  async function loadResults(forceGenerate = false): Promise<void> {
    setError("");
    setLoadingResults(true);
    const response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/${forceGenerate ? "evaluate" : "results"}`,
      {
        method: forceGenerate ? "POST" : "GET",
        credentials: "include",
      },
    );
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      evaluation?: EvaluationResult;
      error?: string;
    };
    setLoadingResults(false);
    if (!response.ok || !result.evaluation) {
      setError(result.error ?? "Evaluation could not be generated.");
      return;
    }
    setEvaluation(result.evaluation);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (session?.state === "completed" && !evaluation && !loadingResults) {
      void loadResults(true);
    }
  }, [evaluation, loadingResults, session?.state]);

  if (loading)
    return (
      <section className="session-entry">
        <p className="status">Loading interview...</p>
      </section>
    );
  if (!session)
    return (
      <section className="session-entry">
        <p className="error">{error}</p>
      </section>
    );

  if (session.state === "closing") {
    return (
      <section
        className="session-entry"
        aria-labelledby="session-state-heading"
      >
        <p className="eyebrow">Closing conversation</p>
        <h2 id="session-state-heading">Interview wrapping up.</h2>
        <p className="note">
          Thank you for participating. Before we finish, share the score you
          expected to receive â€” this is a self-assessment and stays separate
          from the actual evaluation.
        </p>
        <div className="question-panel">
          <label>
            Expected score
            <input
              type="number"
              min="0"
              max="100"
              step="0.1"
              value={closingScore}
              onChange={(event) => setClosingScore(event.target.value)}
              disabled={submittingScore}
            />
          </label>
          <div className="configuration-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void submitExpectedScore()}
              disabled={submittingScore}
            >
              {submittingScore ? "Submitting..." : "Submit expected score"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (session.state === "completed") {
    return (
      <section
        className="session-entry"
        aria-labelledby="session-state-heading"
      >
        <p className="eyebrow">Interview complete</p>
        <h2 id="session-state-heading">Results are ready.</h2>
        <p className="note">
          The actual evaluation below comes from your interview evidence and is
          kept separate from your self-assessment.
        </p>
        {session.expectedScore != null && (
          <p className="note">Your self-assessment: {session.expectedScore}</p>
        )}

        {!evaluation && (
          <div className="configuration-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void loadResults(true)}
              disabled={loadingResults}
            >
              {loadingResults ? "Generating results..." : "View results"}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {evaluation && (
          <div className="results-report">
            <div className="results-score">
              <p className="eyebrow">Actual evaluation</p>
              <strong>{evaluation.actualScore}</strong>
              <span>actual score / 100</span>
            </div>
            <div className="results-assessment">
              <p className="eyebrow">What happened?</p>
              <p>{evaluation.overallAssessment}</p>
              <p className="results-evidence">
                <strong>Evidence:</strong> {evaluation.evidenceSummary}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="results-section">
              <p>
                <strong>Strengths</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {evaluation.strengths.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div className="results-section">
              <p>
                <strong>Weaknesses</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {evaluation.weaknesses.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div className="results-section">
              <p>
                <strong>Improvement areas</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {evaluation.improvementAreas.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div className="results-section">
              <p>
                <strong>Actionable recommendations</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {evaluation.actionableRecommendations.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => void loadResults(true)}>
              Retry evaluation
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {session.lastTurnAnalysis && (
          <div className="question-panel">
            <p className="eyebrow">Latest analysis</p>
            <p>{session.lastTurnAnalysis.analysisNotes}</p>
          </div>
        )}
        {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (session.state !== "active") {
    return (
      <section
        className="session-entry"
        aria-labelledby="session-state-heading"
      >
        <p className="eyebrow">Interview session</p>
        <h2 id="session-state-heading">Interview {session.state}.</h2>
        <p className="note">
          Your completed turns were preserved. Evaluation and results belong to
          a later stage of the session.
        </p>
        {session.expectedScore != null && (
          <p className="note">Expected score: {session.expectedScore}</p>
        )}
        {session.lastTurnAnalysis && (
          <div className="question-panel">
            <p className="eyebrow">Latest analysis</p>
            <p>{session.lastTurnAnalysis.analysisNotes}</p>
          </div>
        )}
        {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
      </section>
    );
  }

  const analysis = session.lastTurnAnalysis;

  return (
    <section
      className="active-interview"
      aria-labelledby="active-interview-heading"
    >
      <div className="sheet">
        <div className="session-header">
          <div>
            <p className="eyebrow">Interview workspace / live</p>
            <h2 id="active-interview-heading">{session.topic}</h2>
          </div>
          <span className="status numeral">{session.duration} min session</span>
        </div>
        <ol className="interview-thread" aria-label="Interview progression">
          <li className="is-active">
            <span>01</span>
            <strong>Question</strong>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span>02</span>
            <strong>Response</strong>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span>03</span>
            <strong>Analysis</strong>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span>04</span>
            <strong>Next move</strong>
          </li>
        </ol>
      </div>
      <p className="session-context">
        {session.interviewType} Â· {session.difficulty} Â· {session.language} Â·
        Question {session.turnCount + 1}
      </p>
      <div
        className={
          session.pendingFollowUpQuestion
            ? "question-panel is-follow-up"
            : "question-panel"
        }
      >
        <p className="eyebrow">
          {session.pendingFollowUpQuestion
            ? "Follow-up â€” from your previous response"
            : "Current question"}
        </p>
        <p>{session.currentQuestion}</p>
      </div>
      <form className="sheet answer-form" onSubmit={submitAnswer}>
        <label>
          Your response
          <textarea
            value={answer}
            onChange={(event) => setAnswer(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Write your answer here"
            rows={7}
            disabled={submitting}
          />
        </label>
        <div className="configuration-actions">
          <button type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
            {submitting ? "Submitting..." : "Submit answer"}
          </button>
          <button
            className="secondary-button"
            type="button"
            onClick={terminate}
          >
            End interview
          </button>
        </div>
        {submitting && (
          <p className="status" role="status">
            Checking answer quality...
          </p>
        )}
        {error && (
          <p className="error" role="alert">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
      </form>

      {session.lastTurnAnalysisStatus === "completed" && analysis && (
        <div className="question-panel" aria-live="polite">
          <p className="eyebrow">Answer analysis</p>
          <span className={`category-tag ${analysis.category}`}>
            {analysis.category}
          </span>
          <p style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>{analysis.analysisNotes}</p>
          <ul>
            <li>
              <strong>Technical correctness:</strong>{" "}
              {analysis.technicalCorrectness.assessment}
            </li>
            <li>
              <strong>Relevance:</strong> {analysis.relevance.assessment}
            </li>
            <li>
              <strong>Completeness:</strong> {analysis.completeness.assessment}
            </li>
            <li>
              <strong>Explanation quality:</strong>{" "}
              {analysis.explanationQuality.assessment}
            </li>
            <li>
              <strong>Reasoning:</strong> {analysis.reasoning.assessment}
            </li>
          </ul>
          {analysis.strengths.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <p>
                <strong>Strengths</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {analysis.strengths.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {analysis.missingImportantPoints.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <p>
                <strong>Missing important points</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {analysis.missingImportantPoints.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {analysis.improvementOpportunities.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <p>
                <strong>Improvement opportunities</strong>
              </p>
              <ul>
                {analysis.improvementOpportunities.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item}>{item}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {session.lastTurnAnalysisStatus === "failed" && (
        <p className="error" role="alert">
          Your answer was saved, but analysis is temporarily unavailable.
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import { type InputHTMLAttributes, forwardRef } from "react";

interface InputProps extends InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  label: string;
  error?: string;
}

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ label, error, id, className = "", ...rest }, ref) => {
    const inputId = id ?? label.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-");
    return (
      <label className="field" htmlFor={inputId}>
        <span className="field-label">{label}</span>
        <input ref={ref} id={inputId} className={`field-input ${className}`} {...rest} />
        {error && <span className="field-error">{error}</span>}
      </label>
    );
  }
);
Input.displayName = "Input";

import { type SelectHTMLAttributes } from "react";

interface SelectProps extends SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> {
  label: string;
  options: string[];
}

export function Select({ label, options, id, className = "", ...rest }: SelectProps) {
  const selectId = id ?? label.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-");
  return (
    <label className="field" htmlFor={selectId}>
      <span className="field-label">{label}</span>
      <select id={selectId} className={`field-input ${className}`} {...rest}>
        {options.map((opt) => (
          <option key={opt} value={opt}>
            {opt}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </label>
  );
}

import type { ReactElement } from "react";
import type { HistoryItem, Progress } from "./HistoryProgress";

export type DashboardUser = {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type DashboardAction = "new-interview" | "history" | "progress";

export type DashboardViewModel = {
  heading: string;
  welcome: string;
  emptyState: string;
  activitySummary: string;
};

export function getDashboardViewModel(user: DashboardUser): DashboardViewModel {
  return {
    heading: "Your next interview starts here.",
    welcome: `Welcome back, ${user.email}. Choose a focused practice session and leave with something concrete to improve.`,
    emptyState:
      "Complete your first interview to start building a personal practice journal.",
    activitySummary: "No completed interviews yet.",
  };
}

type DashboardProps = {
  user: DashboardUser;
  history?: HistoryItem[];
  progress?: Progress | null;
  onAction: (action: DashboardAction) => void;
};

export function Dashboard({
  user,
  history = [],
  progress = null,
  onAction,
}: DashboardProps): ReactElement {
  const viewModel = getDashboardViewModel(user);
  const recent = history.slice(0, 4);
  const hasPerformance = Boolean(progress && progress.totalCompleted > 0);

  return (
    <section className="dashboard" aria-labelledby="dashboard-heading">

      </div>

        </li>
        <li>
          <span>02</span>
          <strong>Interview</strong>
          <small>Think out loud</small>
        </li>
        <li>
          <span>03</span>
          <strong>Analysis</strong>
          <small>See what landed</small>
        </li>
        <li>
          <span>04</span>
          <strong>Improve</strong>
          <small>Return sharper</small>
        </li>
      </ol>

      <div className="dashboard-columns">
        <section className="sheet" aria-labelledby="activity-heading">
          <div className="sheet-header">
            <h3 id="activity-heading">
              {recent.length > 0
                ? "Recent interviews"
                : viewModel.activitySummary}
            </h3>
          </div>
          {recent.length > 0 ? (
            <ul className="row-list">
              {recent.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.sessionId} className="activity-row">
                  <span className="activity-row-main">
                    <strong>{item.topic}</strong>
                    <span>
                      {item.interviewType} Â· {item.difficulty} Â·{" "}
                      {new Date(item.completedAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="activity-row-score numeral">
                    {item.actualScore ?? "â€”"}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : (
            <p className="empty-row">{viewModel.emptyState}</p>
          )}
        </section>

        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 18 }}>
          <section className="sheet" aria-labelledby="performance-heading">
            <div className="sheet-header">
              <h3 id="performance-heading">Performance</h3>
            </div>
            {hasPerformance && progress ? (
              <dl className="progress-strip">
                <div>
                  <dt>Latest score</dt>
                  <dd>{progress.latestScore ?? "â€”"}</dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <dt>Average score</dt>
                  <dd>{progress.averageScore ?? "â€”"}</dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <dt>Trend</dt>
                  <dd
                    className={
                      progress.direction === "improving"
                        ? "is-good"
                        : progress.direction === "declining"
                          ? "is-bad"
                          : ""
                    }
                  >
                    {progress.direction.replace("_", " ")}
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
            ) : (
              <p className="empty-row">
                Performance data appears here once you complete an interview.
              </p>
            )}
          </section>

          <nav
            className="sheet journal-links"
            aria-label="Practice journal areas"
          >
            <button type="button" onClick={() => onAction("history")}>
              <span>
                History
                <span className="link-kicker">Review completed interviews</span>
              </span>
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => onAction("progress")}>
              <span>
                Progress
                <span className="link-kicker">
                  Understand your score movement
                </span>
              </span>
            </button>
          </nav>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState, type ReactElement } from "react";

export type HistoryItem = {
  sessionId: string;
  topic: string;
  interviewType: string;
  difficulty: string;
  completedAt: string;
  actualScore: number | null;
  expectedScore: number | null;
  hasResults: boolean;
};

export type Progress = {
  totalCompleted: number;
  latestScore: number | null;
  previousScore: number | null;
  scoreChange: number | null;
  averageScore: number | null;
  direction: "improving" | "declining" | "steady" | "not_available";
};

type Evaluation = {
  actualScore: number;
  overallAssessment: string;
  strengths: string[];
  weaknesses: string[];
  improvementAreas: string[];
  actionableRecommendations: string[];
};

function ScoreChart({
  history,
}: {
  history: HistoryItem[];
}): ReactElement | null {
  const scored = history
    .filter(
      (item): item is HistoryItem & { actualScore: number } =>
        item.actualScore != null,
    )
    .slice(0, 8)
    .reverse();
  if (scored.length < 2) return null;

  const width = 640;
  const height = 120;
  const padding = 12;
  const step = (width - padding * 2) / (scored.length - 1);
  const points = scored.map((item, index) => {
    const x = padding + step * index;
    const y =
      height - padding - (item.actualScore / 100) * (height - padding * 2);
    return { x, y, score: item.actualScore };
  });
  const path = points
    .map((point, index) => `${index === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${point.x},${point.y}`)
    .join(" ");

  return (
    <svg
      className="progress-chart"
      viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`}
      width="100%"
      height={height}
      role="img"
      aria-label={`Actual score across your last ${scored.length} completed interviews`}
    >
      <path d={path} fill="none" stroke="var(--accent)" strokeWidth="2" />
      {points.map((point) => (
        <circle
          key={point.x}
          cx={point.x}
          cy={point.y}
          r="3.2"
          fill="var(--accent-strong)"
        />
      ))}
    </svg>
  );
}

type Props = {
  apiBaseUrl: string;
  initialView?: "history" | "progress";
  onRePractice: () => void;
};

export function HistoryProgress({
  apiBaseUrl,
  initialView = "history",
  onRePractice,
}: Props): ReactElement {
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<HistoryItem[]>([]);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<Progress | null>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<HistoryItem | null>(null);
  const [evaluation, setEvaluation] = useState<Evaluation | null>(null);
  const [view, setView] = useState<"history" | "progress">(initialView);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  async function loadHistory(): Promise<void> {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-history`, {
      credentials: "include",
    });
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      history?: HistoryItem[];
      progress?: Progress;
      error?: string;
    };
    if (!response.ok || !result.history) {
      setError(result.error ?? "History could not be loaded.");
    } else {
      setHistory(result.history);
      setProgress(result.progress ?? null);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  async function selectInterview(item: HistoryItem): Promise<void> {
    setSelected(item);
    setEvaluation(null);
    setError("");
    const response = await fetch(
      `${apiBaseUrl}/api/interview-sessions/${item.sessionId}/results`,
      { credentials: "include" },
    );
    const result = (await response.json()) as {
      evaluation?: Evaluation;
      error?: string;
    };
    if (!response.ok || !result.evaluation) {
      setError(result.error ?? "Previous results could not be loaded.");
      return;
    }
    setEvaluation(result.evaluation);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadHistory();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setView(initialView);
  }, [initialView]);

  if (loading) return <p className="status">Loading interview history...</p>;

  return (
    <section className="journal" aria-labelledby="history-heading">
      <p className="eyebrow">Personal practice journal</p>
      <h2 id="history-heading">What have you practiced?</h2>
      <p className="note">
        Revisit the questions, results, and movement that shape your next
        attempt.
      </p>
      <div className="configuration-actions">
        <div className="journal-tabs">
          <button
            type="button"
            className={view === "history" ? "is-active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setView("history")}
          >
            History
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={view === "progress" ? "is-active" : ""}
            onClick={() => setView("progress")}
          >
            Progress
          </button>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="secondary-button"
          onClick={onRePractice}
        >
          Start new interview
        </button>
      </div>
      {error && (
        <p className="error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      {view === "progress" && progress && (
        <div className="sheet progress-note">
          {history.length >= 2 && <ScoreChart history={history} />}
          <p>
            Completed interviews <strong>{progress.totalCompleted}</strong>
          </p>
          <p>
            Average actual score{" "}
            <strong>{progress.averageScore ?? "Not available"}</strong>
          </p>
          <p>
            Latest actual score{" "}
            <strong>{progress.latestScore ?? "Not available"}</strong>
          </p>
          <p>
            Compared with previous{" "}
            <strong>
              {progress.scoreChange == null
                ? "Not available"
                : `${progress.scoreChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}${progress.scoreChange}`}
            </strong>
          </p>
          <p>
            Performance trend{" "}
            <strong>{progress.direction.replace("_", " ")}</strong>
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {view === "progress" && !progress && (
        <p className="note">Progress is not available yet.</p>
      )}

      {view === "history" && history.length === 0 && (
        <p className="note">No completed interviews are available yet.</p>
      )}
      {view === "history" && history.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="sheet history-list row-list">
          {history.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.sessionId}>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => void selectInterview(item)}>
                <span className="history-topic">
                  <strong>{item.topic}</strong>
                  <span>
                    {item.interviewType} Â· {item.difficulty} Â·{" "}
                    {new Date(item.completedAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                  </span>
                </span>
                <span className="history-score numeral">
                  {item.actualScore ?? "â€”"}
                </span>
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      {selected && evaluation && (
        <div className="sheet" style={{ marginTop: 18, padding: "20px 22px" }}>
          <p className="eyebrow">Previous results review</p>
          <p className="journal-meta">
            {selected.interviewType} Â· {selected.difficulty} Â·{" "}
            {new Date(selected.completedAt).toLocaleDateString()}
          </p>
          <h3>{selected.topic}</h3>
          <p>
            <strong>Actual score:</strong> {evaluation.actualScore}
          </p>
          <p>{evaluation.overallAssessment}</p>
          <p>
            <strong>Strengths:</strong> {evaluation.strengths.join("; ")}
          </p>
          <p>
            <strong>Weaknesses:</strong> {evaluation.weaknesses.join("; ")}
          </p>
          <p>
            <strong>Improvement areas:</strong>{" "}
            {evaluation.improvementAreas.join("; ")}
          </p>
          <p>
            <strong>Recommendations:</strong>{" "}
            {evaluation.actionableRecommendations.join("; ")}
          </p>
          <button type="button" onClick={onRePractice}>
            Re-practice this interview
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import type { InterviewLifecycleState } from "@/types/interview";

// Purely presentational. It labels whatever state the backend reports â€” it
// never decides what the next state should be.
const LABELS: Record<InterviewLifecycleState, string> = {
  not_started: "Not started",
  active: "In progress",
  closing: "Wrapping up",
  completed: "Completed",
  terminated: "Terminated",
};

export function StateBadge({ state }: { state: InterviewLifecycleState }) {
  return (
    <span className={`state-badge state-${state.toLowerCase()}`}>
      {LABELS[state]}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\components\InterviewConfiguration.tsx

import {
  useState,
  type ChangeEvent,
  type FormEvent,
  type ReactElement,
} from "react";

export const interviewTypes = [
  "Technical Interview",
  "HR Interview",
  "Mixed Interview",
] as const;
export const difficulties = ["Easy", "Medium", "Hard"] as const;
export const durations = ["15", "30", "45"] as const;
export const languages = ["Hinglish", "English", "Hindi"] as const;
export const modes = ["Text", "Voice"] as const;

export type InterviewConfiguration = {
  interviewType: string;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  duration: string;
  language: string;
  mode: string;
};

export type ConfigurationAction = "back" | "accepted";

export const initialConfiguration: InterviewConfiguration = {
  interviewType: "",
  topic: "",
  difficulty: "",
  duration: "",
  language: "Hinglish",
  mode: "Text",
};

export function validateConfiguration(
  configuration: InterviewConfiguration,
): Partial<Record<keyof InterviewConfiguration, string>> {
  const errors: Partial<Record<keyof InterviewConfiguration, string>> = {};

  if (
    !interviewTypes.includes(
      configuration.interviewType as (typeof interviewTypes)[number],
    )
  ) {
    errors.interviewType = "Choose an interview type.";
  }
  if (!configuration.topic.trim()) {
    errors.topic = "Enter a topic or skill to practice.";
  }
  if (
    !difficulties.includes(
      configuration.difficulty as (typeof difficulties)[number],
    )
  ) {
    errors.difficulty = "Choose a difficulty level.";
  }
  if (
    !durations.includes(configuration.duration as (typeof durations)[number])
  ) {
    errors.duration = "Choose an interview duration.";
  }
  if (
    !languages.includes(configuration.language as (typeof languages)[number])
  ) {
    errors.language = "Choose a language.";
  }
  if (!modes.includes(configuration.mode as (typeof modes)[number])) {
    errors.mode = "Choose an interview mode.";
  }

  return errors;
}

type InterviewConfigurationProps = {
  configuration: InterviewConfiguration;
  onChange: (configuration: InterviewConfiguration) => void;
  onAction: (
    action: ConfigurationAction,
    configuration?: InterviewConfiguration,
  ) => void;
};

export function InterviewConfiguration({
  configuration,
  onChange,
  onAction,
}: InterviewConfigurationProps): ReactElement {
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<
    Partial<Record<keyof InterviewConfiguration, string>>
  >({});
  const [accepted, setAccepted] = useState(false);

  function updateField(
    field: keyof InterviewConfiguration,
    event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>,
  ): void {
    onChange({ ...configuration, [field]: event.target.value });
    setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: undefined }));
    setAccepted(false);
  }

  function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextErrors = validateConfiguration(configuration);
    setErrors(nextErrors);
    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length > 0) return;
    setAccepted(true);
    onAction("accepted", configuration);
  }

  return (
    <section className="configuration" aria-labelledby="configuration-heading">
      <div className="configuration-intro">
        <p className="eyebrow">Interview setup</p>
        <h2 id="configuration-heading">Configure Your Interview</h2>
        <p>Select the scope and format for a focused interview session.</p>
      </div>

      <form className="configuration-form" onSubmit={submit} noValidate>
        <label>
          Interview Type
          <select
            value={configuration.interviewType}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("interviewType", event)}
          >
            <option value="">Choose a type</option>
            {interviewTypes.map((option) => (
              <option key={option}>{option}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {errors.interviewType && (
            <span className="field-error">{errors.interviewType}</span>
          )}
        </label>

        <label>
          Topic or Skill
          <input
            value={configuration.topic}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("topic", event)}
            placeholder="For example, Java or DBMS"
          />
          {errors.topic && <span className="field-error">{errors.topic}</span>}
        </label>

        <label>
          Difficulty
          <select
            value={configuration.difficulty}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("difficulty", event)}
          >
            <option value="">Choose a difficulty</option>
            {difficulties.map((option) => (
              <option key={option}>{option}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {errors.difficulty && (
            <span className="field-error">{errors.difficulty}</span>
          )}
        </label>

        <label>
          Duration
          <select
            value={configuration.duration}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("duration", event)}
          >
            <option value="">Choose a duration</option>
            {durations.map((option) => (
              <option key={option} value={option}>
                {option} minutes
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {errors.duration && (
            <span className="field-error">{errors.duration}</span>
          )}
        </label>

        <label>
          Language
          <select
            value={configuration.language}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("language", event)}
          >
            {languages.map((option) => (
              <option key={option}>{option}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {errors.language && (
            <span className="field-error">{errors.language}</span>
          )}
        </label>

        <label>
          Mode
          <select
            value={configuration.mode}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("mode", event)}
          >
            {modes.map((option) => (
              <option key={option}>{option}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {errors.mode && <span className="field-error">{errors.mode}</span>}
        </label>

        <div className="configuration-actions">
          <button
            className="secondary-button"
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onAction("back")}
          >
            Back to Dashboard
          </button>
          <button type="submit">Continue</button>
        </div>
        {accepted && (
          <p className="configuration-success" role="status">
            Configuration accepted. Review the session details before starting.
          </p>
        )}
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}

import type { ReactElement } from "react";
import type { InterviewConfiguration } from "./InterviewConfiguration";

export type ReadyAction = "edit" | "start";

export function hasValidConfiguration(
  configuration: InterviewConfiguration | null,
): configuration is InterviewConfiguration {
  return Boolean(
    configuration?.interviewType &&
    configuration.topic.trim() &&
    configuration.difficulty &&
    configuration.duration &&
    configuration.language &&
    configuration.mode,
  );
}

type ReadyScreenProps = {
  configuration: InterviewConfiguration;
  onAction: (action: ReadyAction) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ReadyScreen({
  configuration,
  onAction,
}: ReadyScreenProps): ReactElement {
  const summary = [
    ["Interview Type", configuration.interviewType],
    ["Topic / Skill", configuration.topic],
    ["Difficulty", configuration.difficulty],
    ["Duration", `${configuration.duration} minutes`],
    ["Language", configuration.language],
    ["Mode", configuration.mode],
  ];

  return (
    <section className="ready-screen" aria-labelledby="ready-heading">
      <div className="ready-intro">
        <p className="eyebrow">Configuration accepted</p>
        <h2 id="ready-heading">You're Ready!</h2>
        <p>
          Review your interview configuration before entering the interview
          workspace.
        </p>
      </div>
      <dl className="configuration-summary">
        {summary.map(([label, value]) => (
          <div key={label}>
            <dt>{label}</dt>
            <dd>{value}</dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
      <div className="configuration-actions">
        <button
          className="secondary-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onAction("edit")}
        >
          Edit Configuration
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onAction("start")}>
          Start Interview
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\hooks\useAsyncData.ts
SIZE: 1293 bytes

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ApiError } from "@/services/http";

interface AsyncState<T> {
  data: T | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  reload: () => void;
}

// Small shared fetch-on-mount pattern so pages don't each hand-roll
// loading/error state for a single GET call.
export function useAsyncData<T>(fetcher: () => Promise<T>, deps: unknown[] = []): AsyncState<T> {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);

  const reload = useCallback(() => setReloadKey((k) => k + 1), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    fetcher()
      .then((res) => {
        if (!cancelled) setData(res);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Something went wrong");
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [reloadKey, ...deps]);

  return { data, loading, error, reload };
}
