FILE: frontend\src\hooks\useAuth.tsx
SIZE: 3457 bytes
============================================================
import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { authService } from "@/services/auth";
import type { AuthUser, LoginRequest, RegisterRequest } from "@/types/auth";
import { ApiError } from "@/services/http";

type AuthStatus = "checking" | "idle" | "loading" | "authenticated" | "error";

interface AuthContextValue {
  user: AuthUser | null;
  status: AuthStatus;
  error: string | null;
  login: (data: LoginRequest) => Promise<void>;
  register: (data: RegisterRequest) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

// Owns nothing about authorization itself â€” it only holds the last identity
// the backend handed back. The backend remains the source of truth; if a
// protected request 401s, that request's own error handling is what should
// react (e.g. by clearing this state and redirecting to /login).
//
// On mount it calls GET /api/auth/me once so a valid session cookie
// survives a page refresh instead of forcing a fresh login every time.
// Status starts at "checking" precisely so ProtectedRoute can wait for that
// call instead of redirecting to /login before it's had a chance to run.
export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<AuthUser | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AuthStatus>("checking");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    authService
      .me()
      .then((res) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setUser(res.user);
          setStatus("authenticated");
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) {
          setStatus("idle");
          return;
        }
        setStatus("error");
        setError(
          err instanceof ApiError
            ? err.message
            : "Could not restore your session",
        );
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(async (data: LoginRequest) => {
    setStatus("loading");
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await authService.login(data);
      setUser(res.user);
      setStatus("authenticated");
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus("error");
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Login failed");
      throw err;
    }
  }, []);

  const register = useCallback(async (data: RegisterRequest) => {
    setStatus("loading");
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await authService.register(data);
      setUser(res.user);
      setStatus("authenticated");
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus("error");
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Registration failed");
      throw err;
    }
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await authService.logout();
    } finally {
      setUser(null);
      setStatus("idle");
    }
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ user, status, error, login, register, logout }),
    [user, status, error, login, register, logout],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used within AuthProvider");
  return ctx;
}

import { App } from "./app";
import "./styles.css";

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
);

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";
import { StateBadge } from "@/components/interview/StateBadge";
import { interviewService } from "@/services/interview";
import { createVoiceSession } from "@/services/voiceSession";
import type {
  VoiceSession,
  VoiceState,
  VoiceStateSnapshot,
} from "@/types/voice";
import { evaluationService } from "@/services/evaluation";
import { ApiError } from "@/services/http";
import type { InterviewSession } from "@/types/interview";

const VOICE_LABELS: Record<VoiceState, string> = {
  idle: "Voice ready",
  connecting: "Connecting...",
  listening: "Listening",
  processing: "Processing your answer",
  speaking: "Interviewer speaking",
  interrupted: "Interrupted",
  error: "Voice error",
  disconnected: "Voice disconnected",
};

export function ActiveInterview() {
  const { sessionId } = useParams<{ sessionId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [session, setSession] = useState<InterviewSession | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [expectedScore, setExpectedScore] = useState(70);

  const [voiceSession, setVoiceSession] = useState<VoiceSession | null>(null);
  const [voiceState, setVoiceState] = useState<VoiceState>("idle");
  const [voiceError, setVoiceError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadSession = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!sessionId) return;

    try {
      const data = await interviewService.getSession(sessionId);
      setSession(data);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Could not load this interview",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [sessionId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadSession();

    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
      void loadSession();
    }, 5000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(intervalId);
  }, [loadSession]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !sessionId ||
      !session ||
      session.mode.toLowerCase() !== "voice" ||
      session.state !== "active"
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const protocol =
      window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss" : "ws";

    const sessionInstance = createVoiceSession({
      url: `${protocol}://${window.location.host}/voice`,
      sessionId,
      onStateChange: (snapshot: VoiceStateSnapshot) => {
        setVoiceState(snapshot.state);
        setVoiceError(snapshot.errorMessage ?? null);
      },
      onTranscript: (transcript, isFinal) => {
        if (!transcript.trim()) return;

        if (isFinal) {
          setAnswer((current) =>
            current ? `${current} ${transcript}` : transcript,
          );
        }
      },
      onError: (_code, message) => {
        setVoiceError(message);
      },
    });

    setVoiceSession(sessionInstance);

    void sessionInstance.connect().catch((err) => {
      setVoiceError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Voice connection failed.",
      );
    });

    return () => {
      void sessionInstance.disconnect();
      setVoiceSession(null);
    };
  }, [sessionId, session?.mode, session?.state]);

  if (!sessionId) {
    return <ErrorBanner message="Interview session ID is missing." />;
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <Spinner label="Loading interview" />;
  }

  if (!session) {
    return <ErrorBanner message={error ?? "Interview not found"} />;
  }

  const sid = sessionId;
  const state = session.state;
  const isVoice = session.mode.toLowerCase() === "voice";

  async function withBusy(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Something went wrong.",
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmitAnswer() {
    if (!answer.trim()) return;

    await withBusy(async () => {
      const updated = await interviewService.submitAnswer(sid, {
        answer: answer.trim(),
      });

      setAnswer("");
      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleStartListening() {
    if (!voiceSession) {
      setVoiceError("Voice session is not connected yet.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setVoiceError(null);
      await voiceSession.startListening();
    } catch (err) {
      setVoiceError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Microphone could not be started.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleStopListening() {
    if (!voiceSession) return;

    try {
      await voiceSession.stopListening();
    } catch (err) {
      setVoiceError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Could not stop microphone.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleInterrupt() {
    if (!voiceSession) return;

    try {
      await voiceSession.interrupt();
    } catch (err) {
      setVoiceError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Could not interrupt voice session.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleExpectedScore() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      const updated = await interviewService.submitExpectedScore(sid, {
        expectedScore,
      });

      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleEvaluate() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      await evaluationService.evaluate(sid);
      navigate(`/interview/${sid}/results`);
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="page interview-page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <p className="hero-eyebrow">
            {isVoice
              ? "Voice interview / Live"
              : "Text interview / Live"}
          </p>

          <h1>{session.topic}</h1>

          <p className="muted">
            {session.interviewType} · {session.difficulty} ·{" "}
            {session.language} · {session.mode}
          </p>
        </div>

        <StateBadge state={state} />
      </div>

      <ErrorBanner message={error} />

      {state === "active" && (
        <>
          <div className="interview-progress">
            <div className="interview-progress-step active">
              <span>01</span>
              <strong>Question</strong>
            </div>

            <div
              className={`interview-progress-step ${
                answer ? "active" : ""
              }`}
            >
              <span>02</span>
              <strong>{isVoice ? "Your turn" : "Response"}</strong>
            </div>

            <div className="interview-progress-step">
              <span>03</span>
              <strong>Analysis</strong>
            </div>

            <div className="interview-progress-step">
              <span>04</span>
              <strong>Next move</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          {isVoice ? (
            <div className="voice-interview-workspace">
              <section className="voice-question-panel">
                <div className="voice-panel-label">
                  CURRENT QUESTION
                </div>

                <div className="voice-interviewer">
                  <div className="voice-avatar">
                    <span>IR</span>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <span className="voice-role">
                      I-RIS Interviewer
                    </span>

                    <span
                      className={`voice-state ${
                        voiceState === "speaking"
                          ? "is-speaking"
                          : ""
                      }`}
                    >
                      {VOICE_LABELS[voiceState]}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="voice-question-text">
                  {session.currentQuestion ??
                    "Waiting for the next question..."}
                </div>

                <div className="voice-speaking-bars">
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                </div>
              </section>

              <section className="voice-response-panel">
                <div className="voice-panel-label">
                  YOUR RESPONSE
                </div>

                <div
                  className={`voice-mic-orb ${
                    voiceState === "listening"
                      ? "is-listening"
                      : voiceState === "processing"
                        ? "is-processing"
                        : ""
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="voice-mic-icon">🎙</span>
                </div>

                <h2>
                  {VOICE_LABELS[voiceState]}
                </h2>

                <p className="muted voice-help">
                  Speak naturally. Your response will appear as
                  transcription below.
                </p>

                <div className="voice-waveform" aria-hidden="true">
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                  <span />
                </div>

                <div className="voice-transcript">
                  <span className="voice-transcript-label">
                    Live transcription
                  </span>

                  <p>
                    {answer ||
                      "Your spoken response will appear here..."}
                  </p>
                </div>

                {voiceError && (
                  <div className="voice-error" role="alert">
                    <strong>Voice issue</strong>
                    <span>{voiceError}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="voice-controls">
                  {voiceState === "listening" ? (
                    <Button
                      onClick={handleStopListening}
                      disabled={busy}
                    >
                      Stop speaking
                    </Button>
                  ) : (
                    <Button
                      onClick={handleStartListening}
                      disabled={
                        busy ||
                        voiceState === "connecting" ||
                        voiceState === "processing"
                      }
                    >
                      Start speaking
                    </Button>
                  )}

                  {voiceState === "speaking" && (
                    <Button
                      variant="secondary"
                      onClick={handleInterrupt}
                    >
                      Interrupt interviewer
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="voice-secondary-actions">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="voice-text-fallback"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setVoiceError(
                        "Text fallback UI will be enabled in the next voice UI step.",
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    Switch to text fallback
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="voice-end-button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      void withBusy(async () => {
                        const updated =
                          await interviewService.terminate(sid);
                        setSession(updated);
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    End interview
                  </button>
                </div>
              </section>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Card title="Interview">
              <div className="question-block">
                <p className="block-label">Question</p>

                <p className="question-text">
                  {session.currentQuestion ??
                    "Waiting for the next question..."}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="response-block">
                <p className="block-label">Your response</p>

                <textarea
                  className="answer-input"
                  rows={7}
                  placeholder="Type your answer..."
                  value={answer}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    setAnswer(event.target.value)
                  }
                  disabled={busy}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="interview-submit-row">
                <Button
                  onClick={handleSubmitAnswer}
                  loading={busy}
                  disabled={!answer.trim()}
                >
                  Submit answer
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {state === "closing" && (
        <Card title="Interview complete">
          <p>
            The interview is wrapping up. Before we generate your
            evaluation, give us your own expected score.
          </p>

          <p className="muted">
            Your expected score is a self-assessment. It is stored
            with the interview but is not used to calculate your
            actual evaluation score.
          </p>

          <div className="score-control">
            <input
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={100}
              value={expectedScore}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setExpectedScore(Number(event.target.value))
              }
              disabled={busy}
            />

            <div className="score-readout">
              {expectedScore}
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button onClick={handleExpectedScore} loading={busy}>
            Submit expected score
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "completed" && (
        <Card title="Interview completed">
          <p>
            Your interview is complete. Generate your evaluation
            to see your performance feedback.
          </p>

          <Button onClick={handleEvaluate} loading={busy}>
            Get evaluation
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "terminated" && (
        <Card title="Interview terminated">
          <p>
            This interview session was terminated and can no longer
            accept answers.
          </p>

          <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}>
            Back to dashboard
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "not_started" && (
        <Card title="Interview not started">
          <p>This interview has not started yet.</p>

          <Button onClick={() => navigate("/interview/setup")}>
            Back to setup
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";
import { interviewService } from "@/services/interview";
import { createVoiceSession } from "@/services/voiceSession";
import type {
  VoiceSession,
  VoiceState,
  VoiceStateSnapshot,
} from "@/types/voice";
import { evaluationService } from "@/services/evaluation";
import { ApiError } from "@/services/http";
import type { InterviewSession } from "@/types/interview";

export function ActiveInterview() {
  const { sessionId } = useParams<{ sessionId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [session, setSession] = useState<InterviewSession | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [expectedScore, setExpectedScore] = useState(70);

  const [voiceSession, setVoiceSession] = useState<VoiceSession | null>(null);
  const [voiceState, setVoiceState] = useState<VoiceState>("idle");
  const [voiceTranscript, setVoiceTranscript] = useState("");

  const currentState = session?.state;

  const loadSession = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!sessionId) return;

    try {
      const data = await interviewService.getSession(sessionId);
      setSession(data);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Could not load this interview",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [sessionId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadSession();

    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
      void loadSession();
    }, 5000);

    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(intervalId);
    };
  }, [loadSession]);

  /*
   * Voice lifecycle.
   *
   * Important:
   * This connects the existing VoiceSession abstraction to the UI.
   * The backend/provider still needs to provide the complete STT/TTS
   * pipeline separately.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      session?.mode !== "Voice" ||
      currentState !== "active" ||
      !sessionId
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const protocol =
      window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss" : "ws";

    const sessionInstance = createVoiceSession({
      url: `${protocol}://${window.location.host}/voice`,
      interviewSessionId,

      onStateChange: (snapshot: VoiceStateSnapshot) => {
        setVoiceState(snapshot.state);

        if (snapshot.errorMessage) {
          setError(snapshot.errorMessage);
        }
      },

      onTranscript: (transcript, isFinal) => {
        if (!isFinal) return;

        setVoiceTranscript((current) =>
          current ? `${current} ${transcript}` : transcript,
        );

        setAnswer((current) =>
          current ? `${current} ${transcript}` : transcript,
        );
      },

      onError: (_code, message) => {
        setError(message);
      },
    });

    setVoiceSession(sessionInstance);

    void sessionInstance.connect().catch((err) => {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Voice connection failed",
      );
    });

    return () => {
      void sessionInstance.disconnect();
      setVoiceSession(null);
      setVoiceState("idle");
    };
  }, [session?.mode, currentState, sessionId]);

  if (!sessionId) {
    return <ErrorBanner message="Interview session ID is missing." />;
  }

  const interviewSessionId = sessionId;

  if (loading) {
    return <Spinner label="Loading interview" />;
  }

  if (!session) {
    return <ErrorBanner message={error ?? "Interview not found"} />;
  }

  const state = session.state;
  const isVoice = session.mode === "Voice";

  async function withBusy(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Something went wrong",
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmitAnswer() {
    if (!answer.trim()) return;

    await withBusy(async () => {
      const updated = await interviewService.submitAnswer(interviewSessionId, {
        answer,
      });

      setAnswer("");
      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleStartSpeaking() {
    if (!voiceSession) {
      setError("Voice session is not ready yet.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError(null);
      setVoiceTranscript("");
      await voiceSession.startListening();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Could not access the microphone.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleStopSpeaking() {
    if (!voiceSession) return;

    try {
      await voiceSession.stopListening();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Could not stop microphone capture.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleInterrupt() {
    if (!voiceSession) return;

    try {
      await voiceSession.interrupt();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Could not interrupt voice session.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleVoiceSubmit() {
    if (!answer.trim()) {
      setError("Speak an answer before submitting.");
      return;
    }

    if (voiceSession) {
      try {
        await voiceSession.stopListening();
      } catch {
        // The answer can still be submitted if microphone shutdown fails.
      }
    }

    await handleSubmitAnswer();

    setVoiceTranscript("");
  }

  async function handleCloseInterview() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      if (voiceSession) {
        try {
          await voiceSession.stopListening();
        } catch {
          // Continue with interview closing.
        }
      }

      const updated = await interviewService.submitClosing(interviewSessionId);
      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleExpectedScore() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      const updated = await interviewService.submitExpectedScore(
        interviewSessionId,
        {
          expectedScore,
        },
      );

      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleEvaluate() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      await evaluationService.evaluate(interviewSessionId);
      navigate(`/interview/${interviewSessionId}/results`);
    });
  }

  function getVoiceLabel() {
    switch (voiceState) {
      case "connecting":
        return "Connecting...";
      case "listening":
        return "Listening";
      case "processing":
        return "Processing";
      case "speaking":
        return "Interviewer speaking";
      case "interrupted":
        return "Interrupted";
      case "error":
        return "Voice error";
      case "disconnected":
        return "Disconnected";
      default:
        return "Ready to speak";
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="page-stack active-interview-page">

      <div className="interview-header-card">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">
            INTERVIEW WORKSPACE / {isVoice ? "VOICE" : "TEXT"}
          </div>

          <h1>{session.topic}</h1>

          <p className="muted">
            {session.interviewType} · {session.difficulty} ·{" "}
            {session.language}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="session-status-pill">
          <span className="status-dot" />
          {session.duration} min session
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="interview-progress-strip">
        <div className="progress-step active">
          <strong>01</strong>
          <span>Question</span>
        </div>

        <div className="progress-step active">
          <strong>02</strong>
          <span>Response</span>
        </div>

        <div className="progress-step">
          <strong>03</strong>
          <span>Analysis</span>
        </div>

        <div className="progress-step">
          <strong>04</strong>
          <span>Next move</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      {state === "active" && (
        <>
          <Card>
            <div className="question-panel">
              <div className="eyebrow">CURRENT QUESTION</div>

              <div className="question-text">
                {session.currentQuestion ??
                  "The interviewer is preparing the next question..."}
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>

          {isVoice ? (
            <Card>
              <div className="voice-workspace">

                <div className="voice-interviewer">
                  <div className="voice-avatar">
                    <span className="voice-avatar-pulse" />
                    <span>R</span>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <div className="eyebrow">
                      I-RIS INTERVIEWER
                    </div>

                    <h2>Voice Interview</h2>

                    <p className="muted">
                      Speak naturally. I-RIS will capture your response
                      and continue the interview.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className={`voice-state voice-state-${voiceState}`}>
                  <span className="voice-state-dot" />
                  {getVoiceLabel()}
                </div>

                <div className="voice-mic-zone">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={`voice-mic-button ${
                      voiceState === "listening"
                        ? "is-listening"
                        : ""
                    }`}
                    onClick={
                      voiceState === "listening"
                        ? handleStopSpeaking
                        : handleStartSpeaking
                    }
                    disabled={
                      busy ||
                      voiceState === "connecting" ||
                      voiceState === "processing"
                    }
                    aria-label={
                      voiceState === "listening"
                        ? "Stop speaking"
                        : "Start speaking"
                    }
                  >
                    <span className="voice-mic-icon">🎙</span>
                  </button>

                  <div className="voice-mic-title">
                    {voiceState === "listening"
                      ? "Listening..."
                      : "Tap to speak"}
                  </div>

                  <div className="voice-mic-help">
                    {voiceState === "listening"
                      ? "Speak your answer clearly"
                      : "Microphone is ready"}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="voice-transcript-panel">
                  <div className="voice-transcript-header">
                    <span>LIVE TRANSCRIPT</span>

                    {voiceState === "listening" && (
                      <span className="transcript-live">
                        LIVE
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="voice-transcript">
                    {voiceTranscript ||
                      "Your spoken response will appear here..."}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="voice-controls">
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    onClick={handleInterrupt}
                    disabled={
                      !voiceSession ||
                      busy ||
                      voiceState === "idle" ||
                      voiceState === "disconnected"
                    }
                  >
                    Interrupt
                  </Button>

                  <Button
                    onClick={handleVoiceSubmit}
                    loading={busy}
                    disabled={!answer.trim()}
                  >
                    Submit response
                  </Button>
                </div>

                <div className="voice-secondary-actions">
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    onClick={handleCloseInterview}
                    disabled={busy}
                  >
                    End interview
                  </Button>
                </div>

              </div>
            </Card>
          ) : (
            <Card>
              <div className="text-response-workspace">
                <label className="field-label">
                  Your response
                </label>

                <textarea
                  className="text-response-area"
                  value={answer}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    setAnswer(event.target.value)
                  }
                  placeholder="Write your answer here"
                  disabled={busy}
                />

                <div className="response-actions">
                  <Button
                    onClick={handleSubmitAnswer}
                    loading={busy}
                    disabled={!answer.trim()}
                  >
                    Submit answer
                  </Button>

                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    onClick={handleCloseInterview}
                    disabled={busy}
                  >
                    End interview
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </Card>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {state === "closing" && (
        <Card title="Interview complete">
          <p>
            The interview is wrapping up. Before we generate your
            evaluation, give us your own expected score.
          </p>

          <p className="muted">
            Your expected score is a self-assessment. It is stored with
            the interview but is not used to calculate your actual
            evaluation score.
          </p>

          <div className="score-control">
            <input
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={100}
              value={expectedScore}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setExpectedScore(Number(event.target.value))
              }
              disabled={busy}
            />

            <div className="score-readout">
              {expectedScore}
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button
            onClick={handleExpectedScore}
            loading={busy}
          >
            Submit expected score
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "completed" && (
        <Card title="Interview completed">
          <p>
            Your interview is complete. Generate your evaluation to
            see your performance feedback.
          </p>

          <Button
            onClick={handleEvaluate}
            loading={busy}
          >
            Get evaluation
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "terminated" && (
        <Card title="Interview terminated">
          <p>
            This interview session was terminated and can no longer
            accept answers.
          </p>

          <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}>
            Back to dashboard
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "not_started" && (
        <Card title="Interview not started">
          <p>
            This interview has not started yet.
          </p>

          <Button onClick={() => navigate("/interview/setup")}>
            Back to setup
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";
import { StateBadge } from "@/components/interview/StateBadge";
import { interviewService } from "@/services/interview";
import { createVoiceSession } from "@/services/voiceSession";
import type { VoiceSession } from "@/types/voice";
import { evaluationService } from "@/services/evaluation";
import { ApiError } from "@/services/http";
import type { InterviewSession } from "@/types/interview";

export function ActiveInterview() {
  const { sessionId } = useParams<{ sessionId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [session, setSession] = useState<InterviewSession | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [expectedScore, setExpectedScore] = useState(70);
  const [voiceSession, setVoiceSession] = useState<VoiceSession | null>(null);
  const currentState = session?.state;

  const loadSession = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!sessionId) return;

    try {
      const data = await interviewService.getSession(sessionId);
      setSession(data);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Could not load this interview",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [sessionId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (session?.mode !== "Voice" || currentState !== "active") {
      return;
    }

    const sessionInstance = createVoiceSession({
      url: `${window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss" : "ws"}://${window.location.host}/voice`,      sessionId: sid,
      onStateChange: (snapshot) => {
        console.log("[I-RIS Voice]", snapshot);
      },
      onTranscript: (transcript, isFinal) => {
        if (isFinal) {
          setAnswer((current) =>
            current ? `${current} ${transcript}` : transcript,
          );
        }
      },
      onError: (_code, message) => {
        setError(message);
      },
    });

    setVoiceSession(sessionInstance);

    void sessionInstance.connect().catch((err) => {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Voice connection failed",
      );
    });

    return () => {
      void sessionInstance.disconnect();
      setVoiceSession(null);
    };
  }, [session?.mode, currentState]);
  useEffect(() => {
    loadSession();

    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
      loadSession();
    }, 5000);

    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(intervalId);
    };
  }, [loadSession]);

  if (!sessionId) {
    return <ErrorBanner message="Interview session ID is missing." />;
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <Spinner label="Loading interview" />;
  }

  if (!session) {
    return <ErrorBanner message={error ?? "Interview not found"} />;
  }

  const sid = sessionId;
  const state = session.state;

  async function withBusy(action: () => Promise<void>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await action();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Something went wrong",
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmitAnswer() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      const updated = await interviewService.submitAnswer(sid, {
        answer,
      });

      setAnswer("");
      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleExpectedScore() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      const updated = await interviewService.submitExpectedScore(sid, {
        expectedScore,
      });

      setSession(updated);
    });
  }

  async function handleEvaluate() {
    await withBusy(async () => {
      await evaluationService.evaluate(sid);
      navigate(`/interview/${sid}/results`);
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="page interview-page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1>{session.topic}</h1>
          <p className="muted">
            {session.interviewType} ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· {session.difficulty} ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â·{" "}
            {session.language} ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· {session.mode}
          </p>
        </div>

        <StateBadge state={state} />
      </div>

      <ErrorBanner message={error} />

      {state === "active" && (
        <Card title="Interview">
          <div className="question-block">
            <p className="block-label">Question</p>

            <p className="question-text">
              {session.currentQuestion ??
                "Waiting for the next question..."}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="response-block">
            <p className="block-label">Your response</p>

            <textarea
              className="answer-input"
              rows={7}
              placeholder="Type your answer..."
              value={answer}
              onChange={(event) => setAnswer(event.target.value)}
              disabled={busy}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="interview-submit-row">
            <Button
              onClick={handleSubmitAnswer}
              loading={busy}
              disabled={!answer.trim()}
            >
              Submit answer
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "closing" && (
        <Card title="Interview complete">
          <p>
            The interview is wrapping up. Before we generate your
            evaluation, give us your own expected score.
          </p>

          <p className="muted">
            Your expected score is a self-assessment. It is stored with
            the interview but is not used to calculate your actual
            evaluation score.
          </p>

          <div className="score-control">
            <input
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={100}
              value={expectedScore}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setExpectedScore(Number(event.target.value))
              }
              disabled={busy}
            />

            <div className="score-readout">
              {expectedScore}
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button
            onClick={handleExpectedScore}
            loading={busy}
          >
            Submit expected score
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "completed" && (
        <Card title="Interview completed">
          <p>
            Your interview is complete. Generate your evaluation to
            see your performance feedback.
          </p>

          <Button
            onClick={handleEvaluate}
            loading={busy}
          >
            Get evaluation
          </Button>
        </Card>
      )}

      {state === "terminated" && (
        <Card title="Interview terminated">
          <p>
            This interview session was terminated and can no longer
            accept answers.
          </p>

          <Button
            onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import { historyService } from "@/services/history";

import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";

// Shows only real backend-reported information â€” no invented statistics.
export function Dashboard() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const history = useAsyncData(() => historyService.getHistory());
  const progress = useAsyncData(() => historyService.getProgress());

  const recent = history.data?.history.slice(0, 5) ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <section className="hero-card">
        <div>
          <p className="hero-eyebrow">Practice session</p>

        <Link to="/interview/setup">
          <Button>Start interview</Button>
        </Link>
      </section>

        <Card
          title="Recent practice"
          action={
            <Link to="/history" className="card-link">
              View all
            </Link>
          }
        >
          {history.loading && <Spinner label="Loading history" />}
          <ErrorBanner message={history.error} />
          {!history.loading && recent.length === 0 && (
            <p className="empty-state">

            </p>

                <span>{item.topic}</span>
                <span className="muted">{item.interviewType}</span>
                <span className="muted">{item.actualScore ?? "â€”"}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>

        <Card
          title="Current progress"
          action={
            <Link to="/progress" className="card-link">
              Details
            </Link>
          }
        >
          {progress.loading && <Spinner label="Loading progress" />}
          <ErrorBanner message={progress.error} />
          {progress.data && (
            <div className="progress-summary">
              <div className="stat">

                <span className="stat-label">Average</span>

                </span>
                <span className="stat-label">Completed</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>

    </div>
  );

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAsyncData } from "@/hooks/useAsyncData";
import { historyService } from "@/services/history";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";

export function History() {
  const { data, loading, error } = useAsyncData(() =>
    historyService.getHistory(),
  );

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1>Interview history</h1>
      </div>
      <Card>
        {loading && <Spinner label="Loading history" />}
        <ErrorBanner message={error} />
        {!loading && data && data.history.length === 0 && (
          <p className="empty-state">No completed interviews yet.</p>
        )}
        {data && data.history.length > 0 && (
          <table className="history-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Topic</th>
                <th>Type</th>
                <th>Difficulty</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Score</th>
                <th>Date</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {data.history.map((item) => (
                <tr key={item.sessionId}>
                  <td>
                    {item.hasResults ? (
                      <Link to={`/interview/${item.sessionId}/results`}>
                        {item.topic}
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      item.topic
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td>{item.interviewType}</td>
                  <td>{item.difficulty}</td>
                  <td>Completed</td>
                  <td>{item.actualScore ?? "â€”"}</td>
                  <td>{new Date(item.completedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Select } from "@/components/common/Select";
import { Input } from "@/components/common/Input";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import type { InterviewSessionConfig } from "@/types/interview";

const DIFFICULTIES = ["Beginner", "Intermediate", "Advanced"];
const LANGUAGES: InterviewSessionConfig["language"][] = ["Hinglish", "English", "Hindi"];
const TYPES: InterviewSessionConfig["interviewType"][] = ["Technical", "HR", "Mixed"];
// Text and controlled Voice interaction are available in Phase 2.
const MODES = ["Text", "Voice"];

// Configuration only Ã¢â‚¬â€ this page never talks to the session engine. The
// session is created once the candidate confirms on the Ready page.
export function InterviewSetup() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [config, setConfig] = useState<InterviewSessionConfig>({
    interviewType: "Technical",
    topic: "",
    difficulty: DIFFICULTIES[0],
    duration: 20,
    language: "Hinglish",
    mode: "Text",
  });

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    navigate("/interview/ready", { state: { config } });
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1>Set up your interview</h1>
      </div>
      <Card>
        <form className="setup-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <Select
            label="Interview type"
            options={TYPES}
            value={config.interviewType}
            onChange={(e) =>
              setConfig((c) => ({
                ...c,
                interviewType: e.target.value as InterviewSessionConfig["interviewType"],
              }))
            }
          />
          <Input
            label="Topic / skill"
            placeholder="e.g. C pointers, DSA, HR round"
            value={config.topic}
            onChange={(e) => setConfig((c) => ({ ...c, topic: e.target.value }))}
            required
          />
          <Select
            label="Difficulty"
            options={DIFFICULTIES}
            value={config.difficulty}
            onChange={(e) => setConfig((c) => ({ ...c, difficulty: e.target.value }))}
          />
          <Input
            label="Duration (minutes)"
            type="number"
            min={5}
            max={90}
            value={config.duration}
            onChange={(e) => setConfig((c) => ({ ...c, duration: Number(e.target.value) }))}
          />
          <Select
            label="Language"
            options={LANGUAGES}
            value={config.language}
            onChange={(e) =>
              setConfig((c) => ({
                ...c,
                language: e.target.value as InterviewSessionConfig["language"],
              }))
            }
          />
          <Select
            label="Mode"
            options={MODES}
            value={config.mode}
            onChange={(e) => setConfig((c) => ({ ...c, mode: e.target.value }))}
          />
          <Button type="submit">Review and start</Button>
        </form>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { Input } from "@/components/common/Input";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";

export function Login() {
  const { login, status, error } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  const from = (location.state as { from?: Location })?.from?.pathname ?? "/dashboard";

  async function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      await login({ email, password });
      navigate(from, { replace: true });
    } catch {
      // error is already surfaced via auth state
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="auth-page">
      <form className="auth-card" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
        <h1 className="auth-title">Welcome back</h1>
        <p className="auth-subtitle">Log in to continue your interview practice.</p>
        <ErrorBanner message={error} />
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          autoComplete="email"
          required
        />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          autoComplete="current-password"
          required
        />
        <Button type="submit" loading={status === "loading"}>
          Log in
        </Button>
        <p className="auth-switch">
          New here? <Link to="/register">Create an account</Link>
        </p>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useAsyncData } from "@/hooks/useAsyncData";

FILE: frontend\src\pages\Ready.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";
import { interviewService } from "@/services/interview";
import { ApiError } from "@/services/http";
import type { InterviewSessionConfig } from "@/types/interview";

// Displays the chosen configuration and hands off to the Session Engine.
// The frontend does not decide the session's initial state â€” whatever
// createSession() returns is what gets rendered next.
export function Ready() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const config = (location.state as { config?: InterviewSessionConfig })
    ?.config;
  const [starting, setStarting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!config) {
    navigate("/interview/setup", { replace: true });
    return null;
  }

  async function handleStart() {
    setStarting(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const session = await interviewService.createSession(
        config as InterviewSessionConfig,
      );
      navigate(`/interview/${session.id}`, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Could not start the interview",
      );
      setStarting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1>Ready to begin?</h1>
      </div>
      <Card title="Your configuration">
        <ErrorBanner message={error} />
        <dl className="config-summary">
          <div>
            <dt>Type</dt>
            <dd>{config.interviewType}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Topic</dt>
            <dd>{config.topic}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Difficulty</dt>
            <dd>{config.difficulty}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Duration</dt>
            <dd>{config.duration} min</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Language</dt>
            <dd>{config.language}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Mode</dt>
            <dd>{config.mode}</dd>
          </div>
        </dl>
        <div className="ready-actions">
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => navigate("/interview/setup", { state: { config } })}
          >
            Edit configuration
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={handleStart} loading={starting}>
            Start interview
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { Input } from "@/components/common/Input";
import { Button } from "@/components/common/Button";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/components/feedback/ErrorBanner";

export function Register() {
  const { register, status, error } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  async function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      await register({ email, password });
      navigate("/dashboard", { replace: true });
    } catch {
      // error is already surfaced via auth state
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="auth-page">
      <form className="auth-card" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
        <h1 className="auth-title">Create your account</h1>
        <p className="auth-subtitle">
          Your private space to practice and track interviews.
        </p>
        <ErrorBanner message={error} />
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          autoComplete="email"
          required
        />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          autoComplete="new-password"
          required
        />
        <Button type="submit" loading={status === "loading"}>
          Create account
        </Button>
        <p className="auth-switch">
          Already have an account? <Link to="/login">Log in</Link>
        </p>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAsyncData } from "@/hooks/useAsyncData";
import { evaluationService } from "@/services/evaluation";
import { Card } from "@/components/common/Card";
import { Spinner } from "@/components/common/Spinner";

export function Results() {

  const { data, loading, error } = useAsyncData(
    () => evaluationService.getResults(sessionId as string),
    [sessionId],
  );

  if (loading) return <Spinner label="Loading results" />;
  if (error) return <ErrorBanner message={error} />;
  if (!data) return null;

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <h1>Your results</h1>
        <div className="score-pill">{data.actualScore}</div>
      </div>

              <li key={s}>{s}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
        <Card title="Weaknesses">
          <ul className="tag-list tag-list-negative">
            {data.weaknesses.map((w) => (
              <li key={w}>{w}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
      </div>

        <ul className="plain-list">
          {data.improvementAreas.map((a) => (
            <li key={a}>{a}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Card>

            <li key={r}>{r}</li>

        </Link>
        <Link to="/interview/setup" state={{ config: undefined }}>
          <Button>Practice again</Button>
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type { AuthResponse, LoginRequest, RegisterRequest } from "@/types/auth";

// Identity Service. The frontend collects credentials and renders whatever
// the backend decides â€” it never determines auth state itself beyond
// reflecting the last response (see hooks/useAuth.tsx).
export const authService = {
  register: (data: RegisterRequest) =>
    apiRequest<AuthResponse>("/api/auth/register", { method: "POST", body: data }),

  login: (data: LoginRequest) =>
    apiRequest<AuthResponse>("/api/auth/login", { method: "POST", body: data }),

  logout: () => apiRequest<void>("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }),

  // Called once on app load so a valid session cookie survives a page
  // refresh instead of bouncing the user back to /login. A 401 here just
  // means "not logged in" â€” see useAuth's bootstrap effect.
  me: () => apiRequest<AuthResponse>("/api/auth/me"),
};

import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type { EvaluationResult } from "@/types/evaluation";

interface EvaluationResponse {
  evaluation: EvaluationResult;
}

// Evaluation Boundary. Starting evaluation and reading results are separate
// calls per the documented API surface â€” the frontend never computes a
// score itself.
export const evaluationService = {
  evaluate: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<EvaluationResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/evaluate`,
      {
        method: "POST",
      },
    ).then(({ evaluation }) => evaluation),

  getResults: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<EvaluationResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/results`,
    ).then(({ evaluation }) => evaluation),
};

FILE: frontend\src\services\http.ts
SIZE: 2326 bytes

// Single point of contact with the Fastify backend. Every service module
// goes through this â€” never call fetch() directly from a page or hook.
//
// Auth model assumption: the backend issues an httpOnly session cookie on
// login (not a token the frontend stores/attaches). `credentials: "include"`
// is set on every request so that cookie always rides along. If the actual
// backend instead returns a bearer token, swap that in here â€” it's the only
// file that needs to change.

const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? "";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  body: unknown;

  constructor(status: number, message: string, body?: unknown) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.body = body;
  }
}

interface RequestOptions {
  method?: "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";
  body?: unknown;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export async function apiRequest<TResponse>(
  path: string,
  options: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<TResponse> {
  const { method = "GET", body, signal } = options;
  const requestBody = body ?? (method === "POST" ? {} : undefined);

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    credentials: "include",
    headers: requestBody ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : undefined,
    body: requestBody ? JSON.stringify(requestBody) : undefined,
    signal,
  });

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  const isJson = contentType.includes("application/json");
  const payload = isJson
    ? await response.json().catch(() => null)
    : await response.text().catch(() => null);

  if (!response.ok) {
    // The backend is the source of truth for error shape/messages. Adjust
    // this extraction once the real error envelope is confirmed.
    const message =
      (isJson && payload && typeof payload === "object" && "error" in payload
        ? String((payload as { error: unknown }).error)
        : isJson &&
            payload &&
            typeof payload === "object" &&
            "message" in payload
          ? String((payload as { message: unknown }).message)
          : null) ?? `Request failed with status ${response.status}`;
    throw new ApiError(response.status, message, payload);
  }

  return payload as TResponse;
}

import { apiRequest } from "./http";
import type {
  ExpectedScoreRequest,
  InterviewSession,
  InterviewSessionConfig,
  SubmitAnswerRequest,
} from "@/types/interview";

interface SessionResponse {
  session: InterviewSession;
}

// Session Engine. Every call here returns the session state as the backend
// currently sees it â€” pages render that state, they do not compute the next
// state client-side.
export const interviewService = {
  createSession: (config: InterviewSessionConfig) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>("/api/interview-sessions", {
      method: "POST",
      body: config,
    }).then(({ session }) => session),

  getSession: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(`/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}`).then(
      ({ session }) => session,
    ),

  submitAnswer: (sessionId: string, data: SubmitAnswerRequest) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/answers`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        body: data,
      },
    ).then(({ session }) => session),

  submitClosing: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(`/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/close`, {
      method: "POST",
    }).then(({ session }) => session),

  // Expected score is the candidate's self-assessment only â€” it is stored,
  // never fed into the evaluation algorithm as an input.
  submitExpectedScore: (sessionId: string, data: ExpectedScoreRequest) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/expected-score`,
      { method: "POST", body: data },
    ).then(({ session }) => session),

  terminate: (sessionId: string) =>
    apiRequest<SessionResponse>(
      `/api/interview-sessions/${sessionId}/terminate`,
      {
        method: "POST",
      },
    ).then(({ session }) => session),
};

FILE: frontend\src\services\microphone.ts
SIZE: 1915 bytes
============================================================
export interface MicrophoneCapture {
  start(): Promise<void>;
  stop(): void;
  isActive(): boolean;
  onAudio(handler: (audio: ArrayBuffer) => void): void;
}

export function createMicrophoneCapture(): MicrophoneCapture {
  let stream: MediaStream | null = null;
  let audioContext: AudioContext | null = null;
  let source: MediaStreamAudioSourceNode | null = null;
  let processor: ScriptProcessorNode | null = null;
  let active = false;
  let audioHandler: ((audio: ArrayBuffer) => void) | null = null;

  return {
    async start() {
      if (active) return;

      stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        audio: {
          echoCancellation: true,
          noiseSuppression: true,
          autoGainControl: true,
        },
      });

      audioContext = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 16000 });
      source = audioContext.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
      processor = audioContext.createScriptProcessor(2048, 1, 1);

      processor.onaudioprocess = (event) => {
        if (!active || !audioHandler) return;

        const input = event.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0);
        const pcm = new Int16Array(input.length);

        for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i += 1) {
          const sample = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, input[i]));
          pcm[i] = sample < 0 ? sample * 0x8000 : sample * 0x7fff;
        }

        audioHandler(pcm.buffer);
      };

      source.connect(processor);
      processor.connect(audioContext.destination);
      active = true;
    },

    stop() {
      active = false;

      processor?.disconnect();
      source?.disconnect();
      stream?.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());

      void audioContext?.close();

      processor = null;
      source = null;
      stream = null;
      audioContext = null;
    },

    isActive() {
      return active;
    },

    onAudio(handler) {
      audioHandler = handler;
    },
  };
}

FILE: frontend\src\services\voiceSession.ts
SIZE: 4060 bytes

import type {
  VoiceErrorCode,
  VoiceSession,
  VoiceState,
  VoiceStateSnapshot,
} from "@/types/voice";
import { createMicrophoneCapture } from "./microphone";

export interface VoiceSessionOptions {
  url: string;
  sessionId: string;
  onStateChange?: (snapshot: VoiceStateSnapshot) => void;
  onTranscript?: (transcript: string, isFinal: boolean) => void;
  onError?: (code: VoiceErrorCode, message: string) => void;
}

export function createVoiceSession(
  options: VoiceSessionOptions,
): VoiceSession {
  const microphone = createMicrophoneCapture();

  let socket: WebSocket | null = null;
  let state: VoiceState = "idle";
  let errorCode: VoiceErrorCode | undefined;
  let errorMessage: string | undefined;

  const updateState = (
    nextState: VoiceState,
    nextErrorCode?: VoiceErrorCode,
    nextErrorMessage?: string,
  ) => {
    state = nextState;
    errorCode = nextErrorCode;
    errorMessage = nextErrorMessage;

    options.onStateChange?.({
      state,
      ...(errorCode ? { errorCode } : {}),
      ...(errorMessage ? { errorMessage } : {}),
    });
  };

  microphone.onAudio((audio) => {
    if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      socket.send(audio);
    }
  });

  return {
    async connect() {
      if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) return;

      updateState("connecting");

      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const separator = options.url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
        socket = new WebSocket(`${options.url}${separator}sessionId=${encodeURIComponent(options.sessionId)}`);

        socket.onopen = () => {
          updateState("listening");
          resolve();
        };

        socket.onerror = () => {
          updateState(
            "error",
            "CONNECTION_FAILED",
            "Voice connection failed.",
          );
          options.onError?.(
            "CONNECTION_FAILED",
            "Voice connection failed.",
          );
          reject(new Error("Voice connection failed"));
        };

        socket.onclose = () => {
          if (state !== "error") {
            updateState("disconnected");
          }
        };

        socket.onmessage = (event) => {
          try {
            const message = JSON.parse(event.data);

            if (message.type === "state") {
              updateState(message.state);
            } else if (message.type === "transcription") {
              options.onTranscript?.(
                message.transcript,
                Boolean(message.isFinal),
              );
            } else if (message.type === "error") {
              updateState(
                "error",
                message.code,
                message.message,
              );
              options.onError?.(message.code, message.message);
            } else if (message.type === "interrupted") {
              updateState("interrupted");
            }
          } catch {
            updateState(
              "error",
              "CONNECTION_FAILED",
              "Invalid voice message.",
            );
          }
        };
      });
    },

    async disconnect() {
      microphone.stop();
      socket?.close();
      socket = null;
      updateState("disconnected");
    },

    async startListening() {
      if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
        throw new Error("Voice session is not connected");
      }

      await microphone.start();
      updateState("listening");
    },

    async stopListening() {
      microphone.stop();

      if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        socket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "stopListening" }));
      }

      updateState("processing");
    },

    async interrupt() {
      microphone.stop();

      if (socket?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        socket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "interrupt" }));
      }

      updateState("interrupted");
    },

    getState() {
      return {
        state,
        ...(errorCode ? { errorCode } : {}),
        ...(errorMessage ? { errorMessage } : {}),
      };
    },
  };
}

// Evaluation Boundary owns final evaluation, actual score, overall
// assessment, strengths, weaknesses, improvement areas, and recommendations.
// Expected score is stored separately as the candidate's self-assessment and
// must never be sent into the evaluation algorithm as an input.
export interface EvaluationResult {
  actualScore: number;
  overallAssessment: string;
  strengths: string[];
  weaknesses: string[];
  improvementAreas: string[];
  actionableRecommendations: string[];
  evidenceSummary: string;
  generatedAt: string;
}

// History / Progress Boundary owns completed interview history, previous
// results, and the progress summary. Do not invent fields beyond what the
// backend actually returns from GET /api/interview-history.
export interface CompletedInterviewSummary {
  sessionId: string;
  interviewType: string;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  completedAt: string;
  actualScore: number | null;
  expectedScore: number | null;
  hasResults: boolean;
}

// No progress endpoint is documented explicitly â€” the architecture doc
// says progress is "available through the existing history/progress
// implementation." Confirm the real route and response shape, then adjust
// services/history.ts's getProgress() to match.
export interface ProgressSummary {
  totalCompleted: number;
  averageScore: number | null;
  latestScore: number | null;
  previousScore: number | null;
  scoreChange: number | null;
  direction: "improving" | "declining" | "steady" | "not_available";
}

// Session Engine owns interview session, configuration, current question,
// candidate answers, answer analysis state, follow-up, duration, completion,
// early exit, and closing. The frontend only displays whatever state comes
// back â€” it must never invent a transition between these states itself.
export type InterviewLifecycleState =
  | "not_started"
  | "active"
  | "closing"
  | "completed"
  | "terminated";

export type InterviewType = string;
export type InterviewLanguage = "Hinglish" | "English" | "Hindi";
// Only "Text" is implemented server-side today per the architecture doc;
// the type is kept open in case voice/mixed modes land later.
export type InterviewMode = string;

export interface InterviewSessionConfig {
  interviewType: InterviewType;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  duration: number; // minutes
  language: InterviewLanguage;
  mode: InterviewMode;
}

export interface AnswerAnalysis {
  category: "strong" | "partial" | "weak" | "irrelevant" | "unclear";
  technicalCorrectness: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  relevance: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  completeness: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  explanationQuality: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  reasoning: { assessment: string; uncertain: boolean };
  strengths: string[];
  missingImportantPoints: string[];
  improvementOpportunities: string[];
  analysisNotes: string;
}

// Generic envelope for GET /api/interview-sessions/:sessionId and any
// response that carries updated session state. Treat this as a starting
// point â€” inspect the backend's actual response body and correct field
// names/nesting before relying on it.
export interface InterviewSession {
  id: string;
  accountId: string;
  interviewType: string;
  topic: string;
  difficulty: string;
  duration: number;
  language: string;
  mode: string;
  state: InterviewLifecycleState;
  currentQuestion: string | null;
  pendingFollowUpQuestion: string | null;
  questionIndex: number;
  turnCount: number;
  expectedScore: number | null;
  lastTurnAnalysisStatus: "pending" | "completed" | "failed" | null;
  lastTurnAnalysis: AnswerAnalysis | null;
  startedAt: string;
  completedAt: string | null;
  // Phase 1.5/1.6 (answer analysis + follow-up decision) exist on the
  // backend already, but the exact field names/nesting for what the
  // session response exposes about the last analysis are NOT verified
  // against the real backend yet â€” this is a placeholder shape so the UI
  // has somewhere to show it once confirmed. Update to match the actual
  // response before relying on it.
  lastAnalysisSummary?: string | null;
}

export interface SubmitAnswerRequest {
  answer: string;
}

export interface ExpectedScoreRequest {
  expectedScore: number;
}

export type VoiceState =
  | "idle"
  | "connecting"
  | "listening"
  | "processing"
  | "speaking"
  | "interrupted"
  | "error"
  | "disconnected";

export type VoiceErrorCode =
  | "MICROPHONE_PERMISSION_DENIED"
  | "MICROPHONE_UNAVAILABLE"
  | "CONNECTION_FAILED"
  | "STT_FAILED"
  | "TTS_FAILED"
  | "AUDIO_PLAYBACK_FAILED"
  | "INTERRUPTED"
  | "UNKNOWN";

export interface VoiceStateSnapshot {
  state: VoiceState;
  errorCode?: VoiceErrorCode;
  errorMessage?: string;
}

export interface VoiceSession {
  connect(): Promise<void>;
  disconnect(): Promise<void>;
  startListening(): Promise<void>;
  stopListening(): Promise<void>;
  interrupt(): Promise<void>;
  getState(): VoiceStateSnapshot;
}
